# Code guide

A map of the code of qando: where each part lives, the words it uses, what the main flows go through and, folder by folder, what each piece does and the functions it offers to the rest of the code.

## How this guide is written

The guide is one file with a strict shape, so that a person or a tool can split it into one `README.md` per folder (a mirror of the repository) and find what they need without reading the whole of it.

- **Top-level sections.** _Index_, _Glossary_ and _Flows_ describe the whole project; they become the mirror's root `README.md`, `GLOSSARY.md` and `FLOWS.md`.
- **One section per folder.** Under _Folders_, each folder of the repository has a section whose heading is its exact path (`` ### `apps/web/src/app/features/vote` ``); the section becomes that folder's `README.md`. Generated or local-only folders (`node_modules`, `dist`, `reports`, `test-results`, `.angular`, `e2e/.features-gen`, `apps/api/drizzle/meta`) have none.
- **Every folder section has the same parts, in this order**: **Summary** (one line, the one the index shows), **Purpose**, then whichever of **Contents** (sub-folders and files), **Components**, **Exports** apply, then **Depends on** and **Used by** (paths of other folders).
- **Each section stands alone.** It never says "see above"; another piece is named by its path.
- **Only the public interface.** A folder lists what other folders use (exported functions, classes, types, components with their inputs and outputs); private helpers are left to the code. An Angular component is one entry, whatever files (`.ts`, `.html`, `.scss`, `.spec.ts`) it has.
- **Kept current.** The pull request that adds, removes or changes a public piece updates its section here. `npm run check` (and so CI) fails when a folder has no section, a section names a folder that no longer exists, a section does not name an export of its folder, or a component's row does not name one of its inputs, outputs or models (`tools/guide-check`). It checks that each piece is named, not that what the guide says of it is true.

## Index

```text
.                              qando: a group finds a date that works for everyone
├── .github/                   GitHub configuration
│   └── workflows/             continuous integration (CI)
├── apps/                      the two deployable applications
│   ├── api/                   REST API (Node.js, Express, PostgreSQL)
│   │   ├── drizzle/           versioned SQL migrations
│   │   └── src/               API source: app wiring, entry points, capability tokens
│   │       ├── db/            database connection, schema, migrations, health probe
│   │       ├── email/         the emails' shared frame and templates, sent over SMTP at once or queued with retries
│   │       ├── http/          Express pieces every module shares
│   │       ├── modules/       one folder per API resource
│   │       │   ├── auth/          sign-in by email code, its email, sessions, the session request, sign-out (Better Auth)
│   │       │   ├── date-interpretations/  read the dates and times a phrase says
│   │       │   ├── docs/          OpenAPI document and Swagger UI
│   │       │   ├── events/        POST /api/events: create an event with the session
│   │       │   ├── health/        GET /api/health
│   │       │   ├── management/      manage an event with its token or its owner's session; list the owner's events
│   │       │   ├── participations/  cast, read and edit a vote
│   │       │   ├── public-events/   read an event by its participation link
│   │       │   └── results/         read the results by the results link
│   │       ├── phrase-editor/ the copy edit of a date phrase by a language model, and its limits
│   │       ├── seed/          store whole events: demo data and acceptance tests
│   │       └── test/          helpers for the API's own tests
│   └── web/                   web app (Angular 22)
│       ├── public/            static files served as they are
│       │   ├── background/    the drawing behind every page, in four versions
│       │   └── email/         images the emails show
│       └── src/               web source: entry point, global styles
│           ├── app/           the app shell, routes and every page
│           │   ├── core/      app-wide services
│           │   │   ├── api/       typed access to the API contract
│           │   │   ├── auth/      the session, the private pages' guard, the management tokens kept
│           │   │   ├── i18n/      translations and tab titles
│           │   │   └── layout/    the header of every page and its account menu
│           │   ├── dev/       development-only pages (not in production)
│           │   ├── features/  one folder per page or flow
│           │   │   ├── create/    the event creation wizard
│           │   │   ├── manage/    the management page
│           │   │   ├── my-events/ "Mis eventos", the signed-in account's events
│           │   │   ├── not-found/ unknown address
│           │   │   ├── results/   the results page
│           │   │   ├── sign-in/   the sign-in page
│           │   │   └── vote/      the voting page
│           │   └── shared/    pieces several pages use
│           │       └── ui/        small UI components and directives
│           ├── i18n/          translation catalogues
│           ├── styles/        design tokens, base styles and their checks
│           └── test/          helpers for the web's own tests
├── packages/                  code shared by the API and the web
│   ├── contracts/             the API contract
│   │   └── src/               endpoints, Zod schemas, OpenAPI generation
│   │       ├── auth/              the sign-in, sign-out and session endpoints
│   │       ├── date-interpretations/  the endpoint that reads a date phrase
│   │       ├── health/            the health endpoint
│   │       ├── management/        creating an event, the endpoints opened with a management token, the account's events
│   │       ├── public-events/     endpoints opened with a participation link
│   │       └── results/           the results endpoint
│   ├── date-reader/           reads dates written in plain language
│   │   └── src/               the reader, its grammar and calendar, the check of an edit, the evaluation set
│   │       └── vocabulary/        the words of each language
│   └── domain/                business rules
│       └── src/               event details, event state, vote rules and results
├── tools/                     scripts that check the repository
│   └── guide-check/           checks this guide against the code, and that no file points outside
│       └── src/               the two checks and their commands
├── e2e/                       acceptance tests (Playwright + Gherkin)
│   ├── features/              one Gherkin feature per user story
│   ├── mailbox/               where the acceptance run's emails land
│   └── steps/                 step definitions and fixtures
└── docs/                      planning and design documents
    └── mockups/               screen mockups
```

## Glossary

The words of the product and the name each one has in the code.

| Term                    | In the code                                                                                                                | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Event                   | `events` table, `PublicEvent`, `StoredPublicEvent`                                                                         | What an organizer creates so a group can choose a date: a title, the options, a deadline, a time zone.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Organizer               | `organizerName`                                                                                                            | The person who creates the event and later confirms a date.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Account, owner          | `users` table, `ownerId`                                                                                                   | An organizer's account, created verified by the first sign-in code of an email; every event belongs to one (its owner).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Session                 | `sessions` table                                                                                                           | One signed-in browser of an account, carried by the session cookie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Sign-in code            | `verifications` table, `SignInCodes`, `signInCodesOf`                                                                      | The 6-digit code emailed to sign in: only its hash is stored, valid for 10 minutes and 5 wrong tries; a new one, once delivered, replaces the last.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Participant, voter      | `participations` table                                                                                                     | Anyone who opens the participation link and votes; no account needed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Option                  | `Option`, `options` table, `optionId`                                                                                      | One thing participants answer: a whole day, a range of days (`endDate`), or a single date with a start time and optionally an end time.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Vote, participation     | `Participation`, `CastVoteBody`, `MyVote`, `ValidVote`                                                                     | One participant's answer to every option of an event. Stored as a participation with one vote state per option.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Vote state              | `VoteState`: `available` · `preferred` · `unavailable`                                                                     | The answer to one option. At most one option of a vote is `preferred`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Answers                 | `Answers` (web)                                                                                                            | The vote while it is being made on the voting page, one state per option ID.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Quick vote              | `allAvailable`, `VoteWelcome.quickVote`                                                                                    | Voting every option available in one tap, without going through the steps.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Named / anonymous event | `anonymous: boolean`                                                                                                       | A named event asks each voter's name (2 to 30 characters); an anonymous one never stores a name (`null`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Deadline                | `deadline`                                                                                                                 | The last day participants can vote, a calendar date in the event's time zone.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Closing instant         | `closesAt`, `closingInstant`                                                                                               | The last millisecond of the deadline's day in the event's time zone; voting closes then by itself.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Event state             | `EventState`: `open` · `closed` · `confirmed`                                                                              | Whether the event accepts votes. Always read through `currentState`, which closes an open event at its closing instant.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Confirmation            | `confirmations` table, `confirmOption`, `ALREADY_CONFIRMED`                                                                | The option the organizer confirmed as the date; final.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Cancelled event         | `cancelledEvents` table, `EVENT_CANCELLED`                                                                                 | What is left of a cancelled event: its links answer "cancelled" with the title.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Capability token        | `newCapabilityToken`, `hashCapabilityToken`                                                                                | A random secret inside a link that grants access without an account. Only its SHA-256 hash is stored.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Participation link      | `/vote/:eventId`, `publicId`                                                                                               | The link the organizer shares; opens the voting page. Its ID is the event's public ID.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Results link            | `/results/:resultsId`, `resultsId`                                                                                         | The link to the results page; the organizer may share it. The participation link never opens the results (`403`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Results                 | `resultsOf`, `Results`, `resultsEndpoint`                                                                                  | What the votes amount to: the winners (best attendance, then most preferred), the alternatives (as well attended, less preferred), the counts and, on named events, who answered each option each way. Provisional while voting is open, final after.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Management link         | `/manage/:eventId#t=<token>`, `openManagementLink`                                                                         | The organizer's link to manage the event; `:eventId` is the participation ID and the token travels in the fragment.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Management token        | `managementTokenSchema`, `X-Management-Token` header, `management_token_hash`, `EventManagement`, `ManagementTokens` (web) | The capability to manage one event, created by the wizard's page before its first send (so a repeated send stores one event); only its hash is stored. The browser that opened a management link keeps its token until it signs out.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Acting organizer        | `ActingOrganizer`, `actingOrganizer`, `ManagementCredentials`                                                              | Whoever a management request acts as, resolved once from its credentials (the management token, the owner's session) before any management use case runs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Recommended options     | `recommendedOptionIds`                                                                                                     | The options the results recommend (their winners); confirming another one asks first.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Vote-edit token         | `voteTokenSchema`, `X-Vote-Token` header                                                                                   | The capability to read and edit one vote. The voting page creates it before the first send, so a repeated send never stores a second vote.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Private link            | `/vote/:eventId/my-vote#t=<token>`, `openMyVoteLink`                                                                       | The link to edit one's own vote from another browser; carries the vote-edit token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Endpoint, contract      | `Endpoint`, `defineEndpoint`, `endpoints`                                                                                  | One HTTP endpoint described once with its Zod schemas; the API serves it, the web calls it, the OpenAPI document is generated from it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Port                    | `PublicEvents`, `Participations`, `EventResults`, `ManagedEvents`, `DatabaseCheck`                                         | The interface through which an API module reaches the database; tests replace it with fixed answers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Step, step stack        | `Step`, `StepStack`                                                                                                        | A page made of steps shown as a stack of cards: the current card, the earlier ones folded into tabs above it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Design token            | `_tokens.scss`                                                                                                             | A named colour, size, spacing or duration; stylesheets use tokens, never literal values.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Demo event, seed        | `demoEvents`, `seedEvent`                                                                                                  | An event stored straight in the database, for local runs (`npm run db:seed`) and acceptance tests.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Session                 | `Sessions`, `sessionReader`, `signedInAccount`, `qando.session_token` cookie, `sessionEndpoint`, `Session` (web)           | A browser signed in to an account: an HttpOnly cookie Better Auth sets, valid 90 days from its last use. It manages the account's events like their management links; a change sent with it from a page of another site is refused (CSRF). The page cannot read the cookie, so it asks the API which account is signed in (`GET /api/auth/session`).                                                                                                               |
| Signed-in account       | `SignedInAccount`, `Session.account` (web), `SessionAccount` (API)                                                         | What a page knows of the account its browser is signed in as: its name (empty until it has one) and its email.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Private page            | `signedInOnly`                                                                                                             | A page only an account signed in opens (`/my-events`, `/profile`); anyone else is sent to sign in and comes back to it. In the API, `signedInOnly` (`apps/api/src/http`) answers `401` to a request without a session.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Test clock              | `testClock`, `X-Test-Now` header, `ScenarioClock`                                                                          | Lets an acceptance scenario set "today" for the browser and the API without touching the system clock.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Date phrase             | the `text` of `readDates` and of `DateInterpretationBody`                                                                  | What an organizer types to say when the event could take place ("todos los lunes de julio a las 20:30"), in Spanish, Catalan or English.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Date reader             | `readDates`, `@qando/date-reader`                                                                                          | Turns a date phrase into the options to mark, by rules alone: the same phrase on the same day always gives the same answer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Date interpretation     | `DateInterpretation`, `dateInterpretationEndpoint`, `POST /api/date-interpretations`                                       | What the API answers for a date phrase: the options to mark, the time questions and the notices. The phrase is copy-edited, then read by the date reader; nothing is stored.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Read option             | `ReadOption`, `ReadDates`, `ReadTime`                                                                                      | One option read from a date phrase: a day or a range of days, with a time, a range of times or none. Not yet an option of an event.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Time question           | `TimeQuestion`, `ReadTime` of kind `question`                                                                              | An hour whose half of the day was not said ("a las 8"): the answer carries one question per distinct time, with its words, every date it is asked of and both readings (08:00 and 20:00), and each option of that time points at its question, for the organizer to pick.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Fragment                | `fragment` of a notice or a question                                                                                       | Words of the date phrase, quoted as written, that a notice or a question speaks of («por la tarde», «el 31 de junio»).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Notice                  | `ReadNotice`, `NoticeKind`                                                                                                 | One thing the organizer is told about a fragment of the date phrase; an answer carries every one that applies, in the phrase's order. About days that are marked, with their dates: `timeUnclear` · `tooLate` · `dayNotWritten` (midnight said of a day is the next day at 00:00). About what gave no option: `unclear` · `mismatch` · `past` (a date already past) · `pastTime` (a time of today already past) · `horizon` · `tooMany`. The reader never guesses. |
| Region                  | `region` of `readDates`                                                                                                    | The region of the organizer's browser ("ES", "US"): it says whether a date in figures ("7/4") is day/month or month/day.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Vocabulary, term        | `vocabulary()`, `Term`, `Language`                                                                                         | The words the date reader knows, language by language, each phrase with the term it means to the grammar (a weekday, a month, "every", "except"…). The grammar reads terms and never names a word.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Text editor, copy edit  | `phraseEditor`, `EditPhrase`, `CopyEdit`, `geminiEditor`                                                                   | The language model that rewrites a date phrase into a well-written one in the same language before the date reader reads it (typos, glued words, chat abbreviations, dictation). It never reads dates, has 3 seconds, and its answer is used only if it passes the edit check; otherwise the phrase is read as written.                                                                                                                                            |
| Edit check              | `keepsWhen`                                                                                                                | Whether a copy-edited phrase still says when as the phrase the organizer wrote: no number added or changed, the weekdays in their order, the only month, the negations, the week and month references, the half of the day and the meal words kept, and no weekday, month, "today", "tomorrow" or half of the day the original does not say. An edit that fails it is discarded.                                                                                   |
| Token                   | `Token`, `tokenize`                                                                                                        | A piece of a date phrase as the grammar reads it: a term of the vocabulary, a figure (a date, a clock time, a number), a pause or a word nothing reads, with its place in the phrase.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Reading                 | `Reading`, `Item`, `Expr`                                                                                                  | What a date phrase names, as said, before any calendar arithmetic: its items (a date expression with its times and exceptions) and what was not understood. The calendar turns it into options from today.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Reading rule            | `rules` in `evaluation-set.json`, `R1`…`R34` in comments                                                                   | One decision about how a date phrase is read ("a weekend gives one range option, Saturday to Sunday"), numbered; the code names the rule it applies.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Evaluation set          | `evaluation-set.json`, `evaluation-set.test.ts`                                                                            | 687 date phrases (487 in Spanish, 139 in English, 61 in Catalan), each with the day it is written on, the region and the answer expected. The agreed score is 100 %: the unit tests, and so CI, fail if one stops passing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

## Flows

What each user-visible flow goes through, from the page to the database. Paths are relative to the repository root.

### Open the voting page

1. The participant opens the participation link `/vote/:eventId`; the route (`apps/web/src/app`) loads **`VotePage`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/vote`).
2. `VotePage` reads the event with `apiResource(publicEventEndpoint)` (`apps/web/src/app/core/api`), which calls `GET /api/public/events/{id}` (`packages/contracts/src/public-events`).
3. The API's **`publicEventsRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/public-events`) finds the event through the `PublicEvents` port (its repository reads only what participants may see) and answers it with its current state from **`currentState`** (`packages/domain/src`). An unknown link answers `404 NOT_FOUND`, a cancelled event `410 EVENT_CANCELLED` with its title.
4. At the same time, **`MyVotes.stored`** reads the vote this browser holds for the event, if any (`GET …/participations/mine` with its vote-edit token), so the page can say "you already voted" and start from those answers.
5. A link that no longer takes votes shows **`VoteEnded`** in place of the steps: closed (saying whether this browser's vote was registered), the confirmed date, or cancelled under the kept title (`410`). A link of no event (`404`, or `400` when malformed) shows **`NotFoundCard`** (`apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`); only a failed request offers to try again (`ErrorState`).

### Vote

1. On **`VoteWelcome`** (step 1) the participant types a name on a named event, then either sends a **quick vote** (every option available) or chooses the dates.
2. **`VoteDays`** (step 2) and, in an event with times, **`VoteTimes`** (step 3) change the answers with the functions of `answers.ts` (`toggled`, `withPreferred`, `dayToggled`); all in `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`.
3. **`VoteConfirm`** shows what the answers amount to (`summaryOf`, `packages/domain/src`) and sends them.
4. **`MyVotes.cast`** creates the vote-edit token before the first send and sends `POST /api/public/events/{id}/participations` with it in `X-Vote-Token`.
5. The API's **`participationsRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/participations`) checks, inside one transaction that locks the event, that the event is open (`currentState`) and that the vote is valid (`validateVote`), and stores it under the token's hash (`hashCapabilityToken`, `apps/api/src/capability-token.ts`). Sending the same token again stores nothing new and gets the same answer. A closed event answers `409 VOTING_CLOSED`; an invalid vote `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` with every problem.
6. **`VoteThanks`** shows the private link `/vote/:eventId/my-vote#t=<token>`.
7. A vote refused because voting closed meanwhile (`409`) makes the page read the event again (the steps stay, locked): it turns into the confirmed `VoteEnded` when the organizer confirmed a date, else (or when the read fails) into the closed one. Either says the vote did not arrive in time, or, when `MyVotes.stored` reads a vote stored before, that the changes did not and that vote is kept; a `410` turns it into the cancelled one.

### Edit my vote

1. From the same browser, the voting page finds the stored vote (`MyVotes.stored`) and the steps start from its answers (`resumed`).
2. From another browser, the participant opens the private link: **`openMyVoteLink`** hands the token to `MyVotes.claim` and opens the voting page without the token in the address bar.
3. Sending calls `PUT /api/public/events/{id}/participations/mine` with the token; `participationsRouter` replaces the name and every state of that participation. A token of another event answers `403 FORBIDDEN`.

### Open the results

1. Anyone with the results link opens `/results/:resultsId`; the route (`apps/web/src/app`) loads **`ResultsPage`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/results`).
2. `ResultsPage` reads the results with `apiResource(resultsEndpoint)` (`apps/web/src/app/core/api`), which calls `GET /api/results/{resultsId}` (`packages/contracts/src/results`).
3. The API's **`resultsRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/results`) runs **`readResults`**: it finds the event with its votes through the `EventResults` port, works out the winners and alternatives with `resultsOf` (`packages/domain/src`) and adds the names on named events. The participation link answers `403 FORBIDDEN`, a cancelled event `410 EVENT_CANCELLED`, an unknown link `404 NOT_FOUND`.
4. The page names each option with **`OptionLabels`** and draws the bars with **`ResultsChart`**; **`ShareLinks`** (`apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) shares the link. A `403`, `404` or `400` shows `NotFoundCard`, a `410` the cancelled `VoteEnded`.

### Sign in with an email code

1. Without a session, **`AppHeader`** (`apps/web/src/app/core/layout`) links to `/sign-in` with the page to come back to. **`SignInPage`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/sign-in`) checks the email against the contract and sends it to `POST /api/auth/sign-in-code` (`packages/contracts/src/auth`). The API's **`signInRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/auth`) first refuses a request from a page of another site, or without `Origin`, with **`ownPagesOnly`** (`apps/api/src/http`): `403 FORBIDDEN`, session or not. Then **`requestLimit`** (`apps/api/src/http`) allows 20 codes an hour per client address and **`sendQuota`** (`apps/api/src/http`), per email, a new code only after 30 seconds and at most 5 in any hour, by the API's clock (a code the email server refused gives its place back, one delivered after the browser left keeps it); beyond, `429 RATE_LIMITED` with `Retry-After`. A broken email answers `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (`contractBroken`).
2. **`SignInCodes.send`** (`signInCodesOf`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth`) has Better Auth create a 6-digit code for the email, stored hashed, renders it with **`signInCodeEmail`** (the code in the subject; the account's stored name, if it has one) and hands it to the `SendEmail` port, **`smtpEmail`** (`apps/api/src/email`). The answer is `200 { sent: true, resendAfterSeconds: 30 }`; an email server that refused it or could not be reached is logged without its message and answers `502 CODE_NOT_DELIVERED`, and the page may ask again at once; the last delivered code stays the stored one until the new one is delivered, so it is still accepted with its own tries and expiry.
3. **`SignInCode`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/sign-in`) keeps the 6 digits of what is typed or pasted and sends `POST /api/auth/sign-in` with `{ email, code }` (from the creation wizard, also step 1's name) as soon as the sixth is in, behind the same `ownPagesOnly` check and at most 30 tries per 15 minutes per address (`429`); it shows each refusal, and asks for a new code after the wait.
4. **`SignInCodes.signIn`** has Better Auth check the code. The right one uses it up, opens a session (creating the account, already verified and with the name sent, if any, when the email has none; the API checks that name by the organizer's rule, `organizerNameProblem`, `packages/domain/src`, before trying the code) and answers `200 { signedIn: true }` with the session cookie. Otherwise `400`: `WRONG_CODE` with `attemptsLeft` (1 to 4, read from the code Better Auth stores), `CODE_EXPIRED` (it expired, was used, or was never sent) or `TOO_MANY_ATTEMPTS` (void after 5 wrong tries, even for the right code). No answer is cached. After `200`, `SignInCode` has **`Session`** (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) ask the API for the account again (`Session.refresh`), so the header shows the account's menu, and only then `SignInPage` opens `returnTarget(returnTo)` (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`; "Mis eventos" by default) in place of itself.

### Know who is signed in

1. When the app starts, **`Session`** (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) calls `GET /api/auth/session` (`packages/contracts/src/auth`) through `ApiClient` (`apps/web/src/app/core/api`): the page cannot read the session cookie itself.
2. The API's **`sessionRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/auth`) answers a request without the cookie `200 null` with no database work (`Sessions.carried`, `apps/api/src/http`). With the cookie it reads the session through the `Sessions` port (`sessionsOf`), sends back the cookie renewed while reading it and answers `200 { name, email }`, or `200 null` for a session that expired or was signed out; never `401`, never cached. A session that could not be read answers `500`.
3. `Session.account` is `undefined` until that answer, then the account or `null`; a request that failed counts as no session, and is asked again the next time a page needs to know (`Session.signedIn`).
4. **`AppHeader`** (`apps/web/src/app/core/layout`) shows **`AccountMenu`** for an account, the "Entrar" icon for `null`, and neither while `undefined`, so "Entrar" never shows for a moment to someone signed in.

### Open a private page without a session

1. The routes `/my-events` and `/profile` (`apps/web/src/app`) run the guard **`signedInOnly`** (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`), which waits for `Session.signedIn()`.
2. With an account signed in the page opens. Otherwise the guard redirects to `/sign-in?returnTo=<the page asked for>` (`signInFrom`), and signing in comes back to that page (`returnTarget`), as in _Sign in with an email code_.

### Sign out

1. In the header, **`AccountMenu`** (`apps/web/src/app/core/layout`) opens from the round button with the account's initial; "Cerrar sesión" closes the menu and calls **`Session.signOut`** (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`).
2. `Session.signOut` sends `POST /api/auth/sign-out` (`packages/contracts/src/auth`); the API's **`signOutRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/auth`) deletes the session, then clears its cookies.
3. Only after that `200` the browser forgets the account (`Session.account` is `null`) and every management token it keeps (**`ManagementTokens.forgetAll`**), also those of other accounts' events opened there: a shared computer keeps no way into anyone's events, and the links stay in their emails. The voters' vote-edit tokens are kept.
4. `AccountMenu` opens the home page and shows the toast "Has cerrado sesión." (`Toaster`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`); the header shows "Entrar" again.
5. When the server could not end the session (`500`, or the request did not get through), `Session.signOut` rejects with nothing forgotten: the page stays as it is, still signed in, and the toast says "No se ha podido cerrar sesión. Inténtalo de nuevo."

### Create an event

1. On the home page, **`CreatePage`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/create`) holds every step's answers, kept in the tab's `sessionStorage` so a reload finds them (`openingDraft`, `keepDraft`, `wizard-draft.ts`): the name and the title (**`WizardDetails`**), the dates (**`WizardDates`**), their times and summary (**`WizardTimes`**, **`WizardSummary`**) and the last step's (**`WizardFinish`**): the deadline, suggested by `suggestedDeadline` (`packages/domain/src`) until it is chosen in **`DeadlineField`**, a note and anonymous voting.
2. Without a session, **`WizardFinish`** first asks for the email and sends it a code (`POST /api/auth/sign-in-code`); the code, typed on the step in place of its fields, signs in as in _Sign in with an email code_ (step 1's name for a new account), and the event is then sent as for an organizer signed in. An event sent once the session has ended is answered `401`, and the account's email is sent a code the same way. While a code is waited for, `CreatePage` keeps it in the draft and the other steps closed.
3. For an organizer signed in (`Session`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`), "Crear" sends `POST /api/events` (`packages/contracts/src/management`) through `ApiClient` (`apps/web/src/app/core/api`) with the session cookie and, in `X-Management-Token`, the event's management token (`newCapabilityToken`): the one it was last sent with when the event has not changed since (`SentEvent`, held by `CreatePage`, so "Reintentar" and a visit to another step repeat it), a new one for an event that changed: every option the dates propose (`proposedOptions`), the browser's time zone, the deadline, the note and anonymous voting. Meanwhile the step stack is locked.
4. The API's **`createEventRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/events`) checks the session and the event (`validateNewEvent`, `packages/domain/src`) and stores it for the session's account with the token's hash (`NewEvents`), its emails queued to the account in the same transaction (`apps/api/src/email`): the summary, due at once (`scheduleEmail`), and "voting closed", due a minute after voting closes (`scheduleVotingClosed`); it answers `201 { participationId, resultsId }`; the outbox the API runs sends it within about 30 seconds (`composeEventEmail`). A send that repeats the token of an event of the same account (its first answer was lost) stores nothing and answers that event again; the token of another account's event is refused (`MANAGEMENT_TOKEN_TAKEN`).
5. The page keeps the management token it sent in this browser (**`ManagementTokens`**, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) and shows **`CreateDone`** in the place of the steps: the participation link to share, the day the results come, "Gestionar evento" (the management page, `/manage/` and the participation ID, which opens with that token) and "Crear otro evento". Any failure keeps the wizard as it was and offers "Reintentar".

### Open the management page

1. The organizer opens the management link `/manage/:eventId#t=<token>`; the route (`apps/web/src/app`) runs **`openManagementLink`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/manage`), which hands the token to **`EventManagement`** (kept per event by **`ManagementTokens`**, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`: in the browser's storage, until it signs out) and opens the page again without it, replacing the entry in the history. Without a token the page opens as it is, with the token the browser held before, if any.
2. **`ManagePage`** reads the event with `EventManagement.read`, which calls `GET /api/events/{id}` (`packages/contracts/src/management`) with the stored management token in `X-Management-Token`; the browser also sends the session cookie, if it is signed in.
3. In front of the management routes, **`sessionReader`** (`apps/api/src/http`) reads the session cookie through the `Sessions` port (`sessionsOf`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth`) and sends back the cookie Better Auth renewed. The API's **`managementRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/management`) runs **`readManagedEvent`**, which first resolves the acting organizer with **`actingOrganizer`** from the token and the signed-in account: neither answers `401 UNAUTHENTICATED` without touching the database; credentials that do not manage the event (another event's token, any other link, the session of another account, an unknown event) `403 FORBIDDEN`; a cancelled event `410 EVENT_CANCELLED` with its title.
4. With the organizer resolved, the `ManagedEvents` port reads the event with its votes; the answer carries the current state (`currentState`), the vote count, the recommended options (`resultsOf`, `packages/domain/src`) and the state of each email queued to the organizer (the summary first), never cached. Every management endpoint shares the voting endpoints' rate limit.
5. The page shows the title and state, **`FinalDate`** (the choice of the final date, or the confirmed one), **`EventLinks`** and **`EventEmails`** (each email's state); `401`, `403` and a malformed ID (`400`) show "No tienes acceso a este evento", a `410` the cancelled `VoteEnded` (`apps/web/src/app/features/vote`); only a failed request offers to try again.

### Confirm the final option

1. On the management page, **`FinalDate`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/manage`) confirms the chosen option at once in the usual case (voting closed, a recommended option, votes in); otherwise its question (`Dialog`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) first says what makes it unusual: nobody voted, nobody can attend any option, not the recommended option, voting will close.
2. **`EventManagement.confirm`** calls `POST /api/events/{id}/confirmation` (`packages/contracts/src/management`) with `{ optionId }` and the management token in `X-Management-Token`.
3. The API's **`managementRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/management`) runs **`confirmOption`**, which resolves the acting organizer with **`actingOrganizer`** first (`401`, `403`, `410` as when reading the event). With the session, `sessionReader` (`apps/api/src/http`) first refuses a confirmation sent from a page of another site, or without `Origin`, with `403 FORBIDDEN`.
4. In one transaction (`ManagedEvents.change`), `confirmOption` lower-cases the option ID, locks the event (`EventChange.lock`: votes being stored finish first, later ones wait and then find voting closed) and checks it: another option confirmed already answers `409 ALREADY_CONFIRMED`, the same option again changes nothing, an option that is not the event's `400` with `OPTION_UNKNOWN`. Otherwise `EventChange.confirm` stores the confirmation and the `confirmed` state, and drops the "voting closed" email unless it was sent (`dropUnsentVotingClosed`, `apps/api/src/email`): it would ask to choose the date.
5. The answer is the event as the management page shows it, read by `managedEventFor`; the page then shows the confirmed date. A `409` or `400` (the event changed meanwhile) makes `EventManagement` read the event again, so the page shows the date confirmed meanwhile; a send that fails says so under the button. From then on the participation and results pages show the confirmed option and votes are refused with `409 VOTING_CLOSED`.

### Retry a failed email

1. On the management page, **`EventEmails`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/manage`) shows a failed email as "Envío fallido" with "Reintentar"; pressed, **`EventManagement.retry`** calls `POST /api/events/{id}/emails/{type}/retry` (`packages/contracts/src/management`), no body, with the management token in `X-Management-Token` (the browser also sends the session cookie, if it is signed in). Any client of the management endpoints can call it the same way.
2. The API's **`managementRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/management`) runs **`retryEmail`**, which resolves the acting organizer with **`actingOrganizer`** first (`401`, `403`, `410` as when reading the event).
3. **`ManagedEvents.retryFailedEmail`** calls **`retryFailedEmail`** (`apps/api/src/email`), which makes the failed email due again now for one more attempt; an email that was sent, is still being tried or was never queued answers `409 EMAIL_NOT_FAILED`.
4. The answer is the email's state, `pending`; the outbox sends it at its next run, and if it fails again it is failed at once.
5. `EventEmails` hands the page the event with the email in that state, being sent ("Enviando…"), and moves the keyboard's focus to it (after a `409` or `400`, retried meanwhile from another tab, `EventManagement` reads the event again); while an email is being sent, `EventEmails` reads the event again every 10 seconds, so the page shows it sent, or failed again, without reloading.

### Read a date phrase

Both sides are built: the smart calendar's box of the dates step (`PhraseBox`, `apps/web/src/app/features/create`) sends the phrase and leaves what it gives on the calendar, after asking about each time said without its half of the day (`TimeQuestions`).

1. The web's **`PhraseReader`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/create`) sends `{ text, timeZone, region }` to `POST /api/date-interpretations` (`packages/contracts/src/date-interpretations`): the phrase (1 to 120 characters), the time zone it is written in and, optionally, the region of the browser's language.
2. The API's **`dateInterpretationsRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/date-interpretations`) first refuses a request from a page of another site, or without `Origin`, with **`ownPagesOnly`** (`apps/api/src/http`): `403 FORBIDDEN`. Then **`rollingHourLimit`** (`apps/api/src/http`) allows 20 phrases in any hour per client address; beyond, `429 RATE_LIMITED` with `Retry-After`. Only then is the body read: one that breaks the contract answers `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` (`contractBroken`). A refused request never reaches the language model.
3. The router hands the phrase, the time zone and the region to **`InterpretPhrase`** (`phraseInterpreter`, `apps/api/src/modules/date-interpretations`), asking for the copy edit: **`EditPhrase`** (`phraseEditor`, `apps/api/src/phrase-editor`) asks the language model for a copy edit of the phrase, with a limit of 3 seconds; when the model is slow, fails or writes an edit that fails the edit check, it answers the phrase as written. A caller that leaves the copy edit out gets the phrase read as given, and the model is not asked.
4. The interpreter takes today (`YYYY-MM-DD`) and the time now (`HH:MM`) from the clock in the request's time zone and reads the phrase with **`readDates`** (`packages/date-reader/src`).
5. The answer is `200 { options, questions, notices }`, never cached. Every phrase gets one: what is not understood comes as notices, never as an error. The phrase is neither stored nor logged.
6. The web's **`PhraseBox`** (`apps/web/src/app/features/create`) works out what the reading leaves chosen (`markingOf`) and the lines that tell about it (`noticeLines`). A reading that asks about a time said without its half of the day ("a las 8") first shows its questions in the box (**`TimeQuestions`**), and is worked out with the answers once the last one is given. A reading with dates goes to **`WizardDates`** through `marked`: the dates take the place of those chosen, the box closes, the calendar goes to the month of the first day marked (`firstMarked`) and the lines show above it (`NoticeBox`); when dates were chosen before, a message at the bottom of the screen says how many were replaced and offers to put them back (`Toaster.offer`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`). A reading with none stays in the box with its lines, and a phrase left unread (`PhraseRead`: `failed`, `limited`) with a red line.

### Check the service

1. Render's health check (`render.yaml`) and the smoke feature (`e2e/features/smoke.feature`, through the web's address, which forwards `/api`) call `GET /api/health` (`packages/contracts/src/health`).
2. **`healthRouter`** (`apps/api/src/modules/health`) asks the database with `SELECT 1` through `createDatabaseCheck` (`apps/api/src/db`) and answers `200` with `database: up` or `503` with `database: down`.
3. The same feature checks that the web's address forwards `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource` and `/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server`, each with or without a path after it, to the API (`render.yaml`, `proxy.conf.json`): an assistant connecting to qando looks for its two discovery documents there. The API does not serve them yet, so it answers `404`; the web's own pages would answer `200`.

### Store demo and test events

1. `npm run db:seed` (`apps/api/src/seed.ts`) or an acceptance scenario (`e2e/steps`) calls **`seedEvent`** through `openSeeder` (`apps/api/src/seed`).
2. `seedEvent` writes the event, its options, votes and outcome in one transaction and returns its links; `storedVotes` reads the votes back for the tests to check.

## Folders

### `.`

**Summary:** qando: a group finds a date that works for everyone.

**Purpose:** The organizer proposes dates and times and shares a link; participants vote without creating an account; the organizer reads the results and confirms the date. The repository is an npm workspace: two applications, three packages and the acceptance tests.

**Contents:**

- `apps/`: the API and the web app.
- `packages/`: the contract and the business rules both applications share, and the reader of dates written in plain language.
- `e2e/`: acceptance tests.
- `tools/`: scripts that check the repository.
- `docs/`: planning and design documents, including this guide.
- `.github/`: continuous integration.
- `package.json`: the workspaces and the scripts that run over all of them (`build`, `test`, `mutation`, `e2e`, `check`, `check:guide`, `check:references`).
- `tsconfig.base.json`, `eslint.config.js`, `stryker.base.mjs`: settings every workspace extends.
- `render.yaml`: the deployment on Render.

**Depends on:** —

**Used by:** —

### `.github`

**Summary:** GitHub configuration.

**Purpose:** Holds what GitHub runs on the repository.

**Contents:** `workflows/`.

**Depends on:** —

**Used by:** —

### `.github/workflows`

**Summary:** Continuous integration (CI).

**Purpose:** `ci.yml` runs three jobs on every pull request and push: format, lint, type-check, unit and integration tests and the build; the acceptance tests against a PostgreSQL service; and mutation testing, one job per workspace, failing below its threshold. The two longest run in parallel parts (`STRYKER_PART`), each held to the threshold on its own: the web's in nine (files listed in `apps/web/stryker.config.mjs`, which refuses a run whose number of parts is not its own) and the date reader's in three (`packages/date-reader/stryker.config.mjs`); one check for each, _Mutation testing (apps/web)_ and _Mutation testing (packages/date-reader)_, passes when all its parts do.

**Contents:** `ci.yml`.

**Depends on:** `apps`, `packages`, `e2e` (through the npm scripts).

**Used by:** —

### `apps`

**Summary:** The two deployable applications.

**Purpose:** The API and the web app, deployed separately; the web reaches the API under `/api`.

**Contents:** `api/`, `web/`.

**Depends on:** `packages/contracts`, `packages/domain`.

**Used by:** `e2e`.

### `apps/api`

**Summary:** REST API (Node.js, Express, PostgreSQL).

**Purpose:** Serves every endpoint of the contract, stores events and votes in PostgreSQL with Drizzle, and enforces the business rules whatever the client sends.

**Contents:**

- `src/`: the source.
- `drizzle/`: the SQL migrations.
- `drizzle.config.ts`: where drizzle-kit reads the schema and writes migrations.
- `vitest.config.ts`, `vitest.stryker.config.ts`, `stryker.config.mjs`: unit tests and mutation testing.

**Depends on:** `packages/contracts`, `packages/date-reader`, `packages/domain`.

**Used by:** `apps/web` (over HTTP), `e2e` (seeds events through `apps/api/src/seed`).

### `apps/api/drizzle`

**Summary:** Versioned SQL migrations.

**Purpose:** Each schema change in `apps/api/src/db/schema.ts` becomes a numbered SQL file here (`npm run db:generate --workspace @qando/api`), reviewed in its pull request and applied in order before every deploy.

**Contents:** `0000_baseline.sql`, `0001_events_options_participations_votes.sql`, `0002_confirmations_cancelled_events.sql`; `meta/` (drizzle-kit's journal, generated).

**Depends on:** `apps/api/src/db`.

**Used by:** `apps/api/src/db` (`runMigrations`).

### `apps/api/src`

**Summary:** API source: app wiring, entry points and capability tokens.

**Purpose:** Builds the Express app from its modules and its edges (database ports, sessions, sign-in codes, the editor of date phrases, clock, error log), and starts it.

**Contents:** `db/`, `email/`, `http/`, `modules/`, `phrase-editor/`, `seed/`, `test/`; the files below.

**Exports:**

| Export                                               | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `createApp(dependencies)` (`app.ts`)                 | Builds the Express app: mounts health, sign-in, sign-out, the session request, date interpretations, one rate limit shared by every capability endpoint (the session reader behind it on management), public events, participations, results, management, docs and the error boundary, in that order.                                                                                                                                  |
| `AppDependencies` (`app.ts`)                         | The app's edges: `checkDatabase`, `publicEvents`, `participations`, `results`, `managedEvents`, `newEvents` (the `NewEvents` port of `apps/api/src/modules/events`), `sessions` (the `Sessions` port), `signInCodes` (the `SignInCodes` port), `editPhrase` (the copy edit of a date phrase, `EditPhrase` of `apps/api/src/phrase-editor`, which `apps/api/src/modules/date-interpretations` calls), `now` (the clock) and `logError`. |
| `newCapabilityToken()` (`capability-token.ts`)       | A new capability token: 128 random bits in base64url (22 characters, safe in a link).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `hashCapabilityToken(token)` (`capability-token.ts`) | What is stored of a token: its SHA-256 in hex, so a copy of the database opens nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Entry points (no exports): `main.ts` starts the API on `PORT` with `DATABASE_URL`, `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` and `BETTER_AUTH_URL` (it refuses to start without the last two; the test clock under `QANDO_TEST_CLOCK=on`), and sends sign-in codes and the events' emails through the SMTP server of `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT` (587 by default), `SMTP_SECURE` (TLS required unless `false`, for a local sink), `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASSWORD` and `MAIL_FROM` (`qando <no-reply@qando.app>` by default); without `SMTP_HOST` it warns and every delivery fails (`502` for a code); the events' emails go out from the outbox, which `main.ts` runs in the same process 30 seconds after each run has ended and stops on `SIGTERM` before the database pool is closed; under `QANDO_TEST_CLOCK=on` it never runs on its own and `POST /api/test/send-due-emails` with `{ "to": [...] }` sends what is due at the request's time to those recipients instead (`204`; `400` without the list), so each acceptance scenario sends only its own emails; date phrases are copy-edited through Google's Gemini API with the key of `GEMINI_API_KEY` (paid tier), and without it `main.ts` warns and every phrase is read as written; `migrate.ts` applies the migrations before every deploy; `seed.ts` stores the demo events and prints their links.

**Depends on:** `apps/api/src/db`, `apps/api/src/email`, `apps/api/src/http`, `apps/api/src/modules`, `apps/api/src/phrase-editor`, `packages/contracts`.

**Used by:** —

### `apps/api/src/db`

**Summary:** Database connection, schema, migrations and health probe.

**Purpose:** Everything that touches PostgreSQL directly besides the repositories. The schema guards what must never be stored whatever the code does: links that collide, a vote for another event's option, two preferred options, degenerate date or time ranges, an event without an owner (an account that owns events cannot be deleted), a second email of the same type for an event, an email recorded sent without the time it was sent (or with one while not sent). The account tables are Better Auth's core tables, with its columns, read and written only through `apps/api/src/modules/auth`.

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                                                                                                                                 | What it does                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `events`, `options`, `participations`, `votes`, `confirmations`, `cancelledEvents`, `notifications` (`schema.ts`)                                                                                       | The Drizzle tables, one per stored concept, with their constraints.                                                                                                              |
| `users`, `sessions`, `accounts`, `verifications` (`schema.ts`)                                                                                                                         | Better Auth's tables: accounts (owners of events), signed-in browsers, ways to prove identity besides the code (empty: qando has none) and pending sign-in codes, stored hashed. |
| `eventState`, `voteState`, `notificationType`, `notificationStatus` (`schema.ts`)                                                                                                                                                | The PostgreSQL enums of event states, vote states, and the types and states of an event's emails.                                                                                                                            |
| `usersRelations`, `sessionsRelations`, `accountsRelations`, `eventsRelations`, `optionsRelations`, `participationsRelations`, `votesRelations`, `confirmationsRelations` (`schema.ts`) | The relations Drizzle's queries follow.                                                                                                                                          |
| `Database`, `createDatabase(pool)` (`database.ts`)                                                                                                                                     | The typed Drizzle database over a `pg` pool, as the repositories query it.                                                                                                       |
| `runMigrations(pool)`, `migrationsFolder` (`migrate.ts`)                                                                                                                               | Applies the migrations not run yet, in order, each in a transaction.                                                                                                             |
| `createDatabaseCheck(database, timeoutMs)`, `Queryable` (`database-check.ts`)                                                                                                          | Probes the database with `SELECT 1`; a failure or an answer slower than 2 s counts as down.                                                                                      |
| `optionFromRow(row)` (`option-row.ts`)                                                                                                                                                 | Turns an option row into the contract's `Option`: an end date makes a range, times become `HH:MM`.                                                                               |

**Depends on:** `apps/api/drizzle`, `packages/contracts`.

**Used by:** `apps/api/src`, `apps/api/src/email`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth`, `apps/api/src/modules/management`, `apps/api/src/modules/participations`, `apps/api/src/modules/public-events`, `apps/api/src/modules/results`, `apps/api/src/seed`, `apps/api/src/test`.

### `apps/api/src/email`

**Summary:** The emails' shared frame and templates, sent over SMTP at once or queued with retries.

**Purpose:** The edge to the email server: the port every email goes through and its one implementation with Nodemailer, the same against Scaleway in production and a local SMTP sink in development and tests. And the outbox the emails of an event go through: queued in the `notifications` table in the same transaction as the change that calls for them, then sent one at a time, each failed attempt tried again after a longer wait (1, 5, 15 and 60 minutes) until the fifth, which records it failed. Every email is written in the shared frame (`renderEmail`) as blocks, which give both its HTML and its plain text, so the two always say the same; the templates of the event's emails, written when the outbox sends them, live here, and the sign-in code's in the module that sends it. Emails are Spanish only until the en catalogue exists, their dates named here as the results page names them.

**Exports:**

| Export                                       | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SendEmail` (`email.ts`)                     | The port: hands one email to the email server; resolves once the server took it, rejects when it refused it or could not be reached (the error may quote the address, so it is never logged). |
| `EmailContent`, `OutgoingEmail` (`email.ts`) | What an email says (subject, plain text and HTML), and that content with the one address it goes to.                                                                                          |
| `SmtpSettings` (`email.ts`)                  | The server's host and port, whether TLS is required (implicit on port 465, STARTTLS on any other; off only for a local sink), the user and password (both or neither) and the sender.         |
| `smtpEmail(settings)` (`email.ts`)           | `SendEmail` over that SMTP server: one connection per email, giving up after 10 seconds to connect and 20 without an answer, so a delivery fits inside a request.                             |
| `scheduleEmail(queries, email)`, `ScheduledEmail`, `EventEmailType` (`outbox.ts`) | Queues an event's email (`summary` or `voting_closed`) to its recipient, due at a time, in the caller's transaction, so it is undone with the change; scheduling a type again makes the same email due again as new, also once failed, and "voting closed" also once sent (once per closing); a sent summary stays sent (once per event). One per event and type. |
| `scheduleVotingClosed(queries, { eventId, recipient, closesAt })` (`outbox.ts`) | Queues the event's "voting closed" email due a minute after `closesAt`, so a vote accepted just before the closing is stored before the outbox counts the votes (it reads them without a lock, which would deadlock with a confirmation); due again at a new closing, also once sent. |
| `dropUnsentVotingClosed(queries, eventId)` (`outbox.ts`) | Drops the event's "voting closed" email unless it was sent, in the caller's transaction; one being sent meanwhile goes only if that attempt fails. |
| `retryFailedEmail(queries, { eventId, type, at })` (`outbox.ts`) | Makes a failed email due again at `at` for one more attempt; attempts keep counting, so one more failure leaves it failed at once. Answers whether it had failed. |
