### `apps/web/src/app/core/layout`

**Summary:** The header of every page, with the account's menu, and the keyboard-only focus ring of text fields.

**Purpose:** The page frame that is not a page, and what every page's text fields share about focus.

**Components:**

| Component                                                | Inputs → outputs                            | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`AppHeader`** (`app-header.*`)                         | —                                           | The logo back to the home page and, at the other end, what `Session.account` (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) says: `AccountMenu` for an account signed in; without a session the "Entrar" icon, which links to `/sign-in` with `returnTo` set to the page it is pressed on, so that signing in comes back to it (nothing from the home page; `signInFrom`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`); neither until the session is known, so "Entrar" never shows for a moment to someone signed in.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| **`AccountMenu`** (`account-menu.*`), `app-account-menu` | `account` (the `SignedInAccount`, required) | The account signed in: a round button with its initial (the first character, in capitals, of its name, or of its email while it has no name), named "Cuenta de <name or email>", that opens a small menu under it with the account's email, "Mis eventos" (`/my-events`) and "Cerrar sesión". The menu closes with Escape (the focus back on the button), with a press or the focus anywhere else, and once something is chosen. "Cerrar sesión" calls `Session.signOut` (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`), then opens the home page with the toast "Has cerrado sesión." (`Toaster`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`); when the server could not end the session the page stays as it is and the toast says "No se ha podido cerrar sesión. Inténtalo de nuevo." |

**Exports:**

| Export                                             | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `provideKeyboardFocusRing()` (`keyboard-focus.ts`) | Text fields show the focus ring only when a key brought the focus to them, never on load, by a click or by a tap. It remembers whether the last interaction was a key or a pointer and, each time the focus moves, marks `<html>` with `data-keyboard-focus` when a key brought it; `apps/web/src/styles` (`_base.scss`) draws the text-field ring only under that mark. Typing never changes the mark. Other controls keep the browser's own `:focus-visible`. |

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, `packages/contracts`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`.

### `apps/web/src/app/dev`

**Summary:** Development-only pages (not in production).

**Purpose:** Pages for reviewing shared components while they are built, such as the step motion. The production build swaps `dev-routes.ts` for `dev-routes.production.ts` (an empty list, `fileReplacements` in `apps/web/angular.json`), so none of them reaches qando.app; mutation testing leaves them out.

**Components:**

| Component                                                                      | Inputs → outputs | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`StepStackPlayground`** (`step-stack-playground.*`), route `/dev/step-stack` | —                | A four-step `StepStack` with steps of different heights, buttons for every change (each step, back, a burst of quick clicks), a lock and a slow-motion switch (the step tokens × 4), where the step motion is reviewed at phone and desktop widths. |

**Exports:**

| Export                                                    | What it does                                                                                           |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `devRoutes` (`dev-routes.ts`, `dev-routes.production.ts`) | The development pages' routes, spread into `routes` (`apps/web/src/app`); an empty list in production. |

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`.

### `apps/web/src/app/features`

**Summary:** One folder per page or flow.

**Purpose:** Each folder is a route's page and everything only it uses. Pieces two pages share move to `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`.

**Contents:** `create/`, `manage/`, `not-found/`, `results/`, `sign-in/`, `vote/`.

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/core`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, `packages/contracts`, `packages/domain`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`.

### `apps/web/src/app/features/create`

**Summary:** The event creation wizard.

**Purpose:** The home page, where an organizer creates an event: the steps as a stack of cards (`StepStack`), the page holding what each step asks so a step that folds into its tab and comes back loses nothing. Step 1 (the organizer's name, then the event's title) and step 2 (the dates, marked on a month calendar that goes from today to twelve months ahead: days one by one, and ranges, days in a row proposed as one option, which can be saved to mark their days again; 60 options at most, and a trash that clears them all) are built, and so are the times of the dates, a step of its own only while times are asked for and a date of its own can have them (a column of hours a day, where each time marked is one more option), and then, in their place in the same card, their summary, every option as it will be voted, on the same columns; and the last step, named "último paso", the deadline in three selects, day, month and year (`DeadlineField`), a note, anonymous voting and "Crear", which creates the event for the organizer signed in (`WizardFinish`) and leaves "¡Hecho!" in the wizard's place (`CreateDone`). The times step and the summary share their window on a row of columns (`ColumnStrip`, `columnsOnScreen`) and the Sass partial of a step of columns (`_column-step.scss`: the label, the row of the years, the window, the row and a column); the summary draws its boxes of times with the voting page's (`_time-blocks.scss`, `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`). The smart calendar of the dates step is built: a switch opens a box over the calendar (`PhraseBox`) where the dates are asked for in plain words; the API reads the phrase (`PhraseReader`), what it gives takes the place of the dates chosen (`markingOf`), and the lines that tell what could not be marked as it was said (`noticeLines`) show in an amber box (`NoticeBox`). Where the browser can take dictation, the phrase can also be said to the box's microphone (`Dictation`).

**Components:**

| Component                                                                                                                                                                      | Inputs → outputs the first column on screen, `place`, is the same for both, kept from step 3 on and the first again from the steps before it; the focus goes to the card when one gives way to the other), and last the last step (`WizardFinish`) in `salmon`, numbered after the steps before it (3, or 4 after the times); holds the name and the title, the dates marked with their times and whether times were asked for (the "Horario" switch, off at first; all in one `DateSelection`), the month the calendar shows (the current month at first, `today`) what the last step asks (`EventEnding`) and the event as last sent with its management token (`SentEvent`, `null` again after "Crear otro evento") and the sign-in code the last step waits for (`CodeWait`). It opens with what the tab kept of the wizard (`openingDraft`), keeps it again on each change (`keepDraft`) and forgets it once the event is created (`forgetDraft`). While the event or a code is sent, and while a code is waited for, the stack is `locked`: its tabs do nothing. Once it is created, "¡Hecho!" (`CreateDone`) takes the place of the whole stack, and "Crear otro evento" brings the wizard back on step 1 with everything as the page first opens (`newDraft`). A field whose value is valid (`detailsProblems`) gets a small green tick at its end (no circle), which screen readers hear as "Correcto". The fields have no placeholder. Until both are, the arrow does nothing, though it stays in the Tab order, and tells screen readers what is missing ("Completa tu nombre y el título del evento.", "Tu nombre necesita al menos 2 letras.", …), never shown on screen; Enter in either field presses it. From 650 px the cursor starts in the name field. | **`DayDrag`** directive (`day-drag.ts`), `[appDayDrag]`, exported to templates as `appDayDrag`                                                                                 | → `dragging` (`DayDragSpan` or `null`), `dropped` (`DayDrop`), `sideHeld` (`-1` or `1`); method `rested(date)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | A drag over the days inside the element, each a descendant with a `data-date`. A mouse begins it by pressing a day and moving more than 8 px; a finger or a pen, which scroll the page when they move, by resting on the day pressed: the element's user says when by calling `rested(date)` with that day (nothing happens when the press followed is not on it, as when another finger has pressed another day since, or during a drag). `dragging` fires when the drag begins and each time the pointer gets to another day (the day under it, read from the page: a finger's events stay with the day it pressed), `dropped` when it is released, wherever it is (its `onDay` says whether the pointer was still over the last day or had left the days), and the click the browser sends after it is swallowed. A press that neither moves nor rests stays a click or a tap. While the pointer rests `SIDE_HOLD_MS` off the days within 10 px of a side of the element, or beyond it, `sideHeld` fires once with that side: the days can change under the drag and it goes on, also for a finger whose day has left the page (its pointer is kept for the element). The page does not scroll under a finger's drag, and another finger on the days is not followed. A drag whose release never arrives, or that the browser cancels, ends with `dragging` as `null`. | **`WizardDates`** (`wizard-dates.*`)                                                                                                                                           | `selection` (model, `DateSelection`), `month` (model, `YYYY-MM`), `phrase` (model, the text of the smart calendar's box, which the page holds) → `back`, `next`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Step 2 inside its card: the label, one month of a calendar (Monday first, no days of other months; its words from `calendarWords`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, its card and grid from the `calendar` mixins, `apps/web/src/styles`) between its two month arrows, a row of tools (the trash, the options counter and, at its end, the "Guardar un rango" button without a mouse or the "Horario" switch with one) and the saved ranges as pills under it, and the round arrows named "Volver al paso 1" and "Siguiente". A press on a free day marks it as a date of its own (`pressDay`), also next to a marked one; a press on a marked day removes it, and on any day of a range, the whole range. A range is made in two ways (`dragDays`): a drag from a day to another (`DayDrag`), across months too, since the pointer resting at a side of the days changes the month: a mouse drags by moving, a finger once it has rested on the day (`LongPress` tells `DayDrag`; meanwhile `HoldRing`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, closes a round ring on the day from outside the finger, over the whole page), and lifted there without sliding it leaves the day as a date of its own; or, with the keyboard, Shift+Enter holds a day and the press on another day makes the range, while a tip over the day held asks for it ("Elige el último día del rango.", a `Tooltip`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, there while the day held is the day used last and on screen, so out of the way once the focus moves to another day, and a press on the part of a day it covers goes through to the day; screen readers hear the sentence from a text that is not seen), the month can change and Escape lets go. Nothing is written between the two round arrows. The "Horario" switch (`Switch`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) asks for times on the dates of their own: it is on screen only while one is marked, off until it is turned on, and comes back as it was left when the last date of its own goes and one is marked again; with a mouse it is at the end of the row of tools, and without one, where that end is the "Guardar un rango" button, between the two round arrows, 14 px before "Siguiente". Either way stops at the day next to another range, takes in the dates of their own on the way and, begun on the first or the last day of a range, moves that end; begun on a day between them it does nothing. The day held, pressed again or left held when going on with "Siguiente", stays as a date of its own. A double-click is two presses. While a drag goes on, nothing else marks or holds a day, nor does "Siguiente" go on (a key, another finger); the month can still change. A range is drawn as one band (its ends as dates, the days between fainter, cut with white dashes where it goes on in another week; two ranges that touch are seen as two) and the mouse over any of its days darkens it whole; while a range is being made, its first day is dark, the days on the way carry a thin ring and the day reached a thick one, with a small arrow at the side of its row the range goes towards. A range is saved with its "+" (`saveRange`), a small round button on the corner of its last day on screen (its last day, or the month's when the range goes on into the next month; it saves the whole range) named with its range for screen readers ("Guardar el rango del 17 de junio al 19 de junio"): the range leaves the calendar, its days free again under a pale band (`GridDay.saved`), and waits under the calendar as a pill ("Mié 17 ➔ Vie 19"; the pills are `SavedPills`, which draws in each what the step's template says and takes from the step the tip and the name of its "−"), the focus going to the day the "+" was on; a range made again over the days of a saved one, from its first to its last (`Drag.alreadySaved`), changes nothing, whatever is marked on those days, and pins the pill of that saved range, which shows it is there already; the click of that same press does not let the pill go. A mouse finds the "+" over any day of its range, though not while it rests on the day the drag that made the range was released on (released off the days, `DayDrop.onDay`, the "+" shows as soon as the mouse comes back); the keyboard, with Tab from a day of the range; without a mouse (`(hover: none), (pointer: coarse)`) a button on the right under the calendar ("Guardar un rango", there while a range has a day on screen) gives way to the "+" of every range, red (`--q-engaged`) and the first with the focus, its row keeping its height: they hide, and the button is back, when the ranges change, as when one is saved, on a press anywhere but on one of them (whatever is pressed does what it does), when a drag begins or a day is held, and on Escape, the button taking the focus; the button is red while it is pressed; no "+" is there while a range is being made. The pills are the saved ranges with a day in the month on screen, under a title that says how to see them ("Pasa el ratón para visualizar estas fechas.", or "Toca un rango para verlo en el calendario." without a mouse). The mouse over a pill outlines its range on the calendar; a press on the pill pins it (`GridDay.viewed`: a filled band over whatever its days have, its two ends bold and the days between faint), until Escape, the pill again, another pill (which is pinned instead) or a press anywhere else on the page, which on a day of the view does nothing more and on another day acts as usual; the month arrows keep the view while its range has days in the month, a day held with Shift+Enter or a drag lets it go, and it is let go by itself once its range is no longer saved; pinned days do not answer the mouse. A pill's "−" deletes its range at once (`removeSaved`), the focus going to the pill that takes its place, the last one, or the calendar: a mouse and the keyboard find it on the pill while none is pinned; without a mouse it is there only on the pinned pill; nothing is deleted while a drag goes on. Each date of its own, each range and each saved range is one option of the event, which takes `MAX_OPTIONS` (60, `packages/domain/src`): from 50 on, a counter in the middle of the row under the calendar says how many are taken (`OptionCounter`), red and bold at 60, when a press on a free day leaves it free and a drag that would leave one option more leaves nothing (`pressDay`, `dragDays`); Shift+Enter still holds a free day (`holdDay`), for a range that takes dates of their own in, and pressed again or followed by a range that would be one option more it stays free. That row is always there, so nothing under it moves when what it holds comes and goes: on its left the trash (`ClearButton`), there while a date is marked or a range saved, which, once its question is answered "Sí", clears every date, range and saved range and lets go of a day held, the month staying and the focus going to the calendar (nothing is cleared while a drag goes on); on its right, without a mouse, the "Guardar un rango" button. The month arrows repeat while held down (`HoldRepeat`) and grow and darken under the mouse, as the other small arrows of the wizard. A finger that slides sideways over the days before it has rested on one (`SideSlide`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) moves them with it, the months beside drawn next to them for the eye only (spans, no buttons), never farther than a month nor past the first or the last month; when it lifts the calendar goes to the month it brought more than a quarter of its width into sight, or to the one it was flung towards, and back otherwise, the days gliding there from where the finger left them (a CSS animation from `--glide-from`; at once with reduced motion); a slide begun while they still glide takes them from where they are drawn. The month arrows, the keyboard and a drag's rest at a side change the month at once, as before. The days before today and after the last day an event can propose (`latestOptionDate`, `packages/domain/src`) are grey and cannot be pressed; the month arrows stop, dimmed but still focusable, at the current month and at the month of that last day. Today is read from the clock again when the step is entered and on every use (a press, a month arrow, a key, a day held, a drag's release, "Siguiente"): after midnight the dates marked before the new today leave `selection` (`withoutDatesBefore`: a range that began before it, marked or saved, leaves whole), a day held before it, or on a range that began before it, is let go, a drag with a day before it (also the first day of a range dragged by its end) leaves nothing, and `month` is never left before today's month. With the keyboard, Tab reaches one day (the one used last, or the first that can be pressed), the arrow keys move by day and by week, into the next or the previous month too, PageUp and PageDown change the month keeping the day, and Enter or Space press the day; each day is named in full for screen readers ("sábado, 20 de junio"), a day of a range with its range ("sábado, 20 de junio, rango del 18 de junio al 20 de junio"). Until a date is marked or a range saved, "Siguiente" does nothing, though it stays in the Tab order, and tells screen readers why ("Selecciona al menos una fecha."). Below 650 px the calendar reaches the card's sides and each day is as tall as it is wide. The "Horario" switch reads the selection's `timed` and writes it with `withTimesAsked`, so turning it off forgets the times marked; the counter and the stop at 60 count the times of the dates (`optionCount`), and the trash keeps the switch as it was (`withoutDates`). The smart calendar is a switch beside the step's label, a drawing named "Generar fechas con IA" for screen readers, off whenever the step is entered: on, `PhraseBox` covers the calendar, which stays in the page under it, unseen and `inert`, both in one grid cell, so the box is as large as the calendar (larger only when its text and notices need more) and nothing under them moves; a day held is let go. What is under the calendar stays: the pills, the row and the two round arrows, though without a mouse the "Guardar un rango" button is not shown meanwhile, and the arrow that goes on is "Generar fechas" (a calendar with a plus), which has the box's phrase read (`PhraseBox.generate`) and says it is disabled, still in the Tab order, while there is nothing to send or an answer is waited for (`PhraseBox.ready`). A phrase that gives dates (`marked`) closes the box: they take the place of every date, range and saved range chosen before, `month` goes to the month of the first day marked (`firstMarked`), the focus goes to the calendar, and what the reading tells shows above the calendar (`NoticeBox`, in a `status` region that is always in the page) until its cross, the trash or the next phrase that gives dates; it is not shown while the box is open. With the box open the trash is also there for a text alone and empties the box with the dates (`PhraseBox.clear`: its text and what it tells go, an answer waited for is dropped and marks nothing when it comes, and the focus is on the text box); with the box closed a text alone has no trash, and the trash of the dates clears a text kept too. When a phrase's dates take the place of others, a message at the bottom of the screen (`Toaster.offer`) says how many, each date, range and saved range as one ("Hemos sustituido las 3 fechas que tenías marcadas.", or "la fecha" for one), with "Deshacer": it puts back the dates, their times and the month as they were before the phrase, takes away what the phrase told, lets go a day held and brings the calendar back with the focus, the box closed. The offer goes once the dates change in any way (a press, the "Horario" switch, the trash, midnight) and when the step folds away; after two phrases it undoes the second. |
| **`PhraseBox`** (`phrase-box.*`), with `PhraseMarked`                                                                                                                          | `text` (model, required: the phrase as written), `timed` (required: times were asked for before the phrase, and stay asked for whatever it says) → `marked` (`PhraseMarked`: the `selection` the phrase leaves, in place of the one before, and the `lines` that tell about it, none for a plain reading)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | The box of the smart calendar: a text box that grows with what is written (120 characters at most, counted under it, "0/120"), named for screen readers and with an example, which takes the focus when the box opens. Enter has the phrase read (`PhraseReader`); with Shift, or while a character is being composed, it writes. Whoever shows the box can have the phrase read with `generate()`, ask `ready` (a signal: there is a phrase that is not blank and no answer is waited for, so `generate()` would send it) and empty it with `clear()`: its text, its questions and what it tells go, the answer waited for is dropped, and the text box takes the focus. While the answer is waited for a spinner turns and screen readers hear "Generando fechas…"; nothing else is sent meanwhile. A phrase that gives dates is told through `marked` (`markingOf`, `noticeLines`) and leaves nothing in the box; when it says a time without its half of the day ("a las 8"), the box first asks which is meant, under the text (`TimeQuestions`), tells nothing until the last question is answered, and loses the questions to the next phrase sent or to `clear()`; one that gives none stays with why, in amber (`NoticeBox`, in a `status` region); one that could not be read, with a red line ("No se ha podido interpretar", or "Demasiadas peticiones, inténtalo en un momento", in an `alert` region). Both go with their cross or as soon as the next phrase is sent. An answer no longer waited for, because the box was emptied or is gone, is dropped when it comes. Where the browser can take dictation the text's well has a microphone at its end (`Dictation`), which writes what is heard into the text; it stops listening when the phrase is sent, when the box is emptied and when it closes, and a microphone the browser refuses is told with a red line ("Permiso de micrófono denegado. Actívalo en los ajustes del navegador."), which goes as the others. | **`Dictation`** (`dictation.*`)                                                                                                                                                | `text` (model, required: the text the words heard are written in), `max` (required: the most characters the text takes) → `denied` (the browser refused the microphone)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | The microphone of a text, a round button named "Dictar con micrófono". Pressed, the browser listens to one utterance in the language of the interface (the Web Speech API; the language is the copy's `create.phrase.spoken`, `es-ES`), and what it hears is written as it comes: after the text there was when it was pressed and one space, each time in place of what was heard before, cut at `max`. While it listens the microphone is five red bars that move, more while words come and until 600 ms after the last one, and the button is named "Detener dictado". It stops with the utterance, with a second press, with `stop()`, when it goes from the page, when the browser gives up (saying nothing, unless the microphone was refused: `denied`), and by itself when the browser, asked to listen, says nothing for ten seconds; what was heard stays written, and nothing the browser says later is. Where the browser has no speech recognizer (Firefox) it shows nothing. A date has its day (`ColumnHeader`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) over its hours (`HourColumn`), and what is marked there becomes the times of that date in `selection` (`withTimes`); a range has its two ends over a box that says "Sin horario", and nothing to press. Four columns are on screen at a time from 650 px, three below, each narrower when the card leaves less room (74 px at 320 px), and two of their usual width under 320 px (`columnsOnScreen`); the step's two round arrows move them one column at a time, on and on while held, before they leave the step (`StepPager` with `held`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`): "Volver al paso 2" with the first columns on screen and "Día anterior" after that, "Día siguiente" and, with the last columns on screen, "Siguiente"; the place of the columns on screen ("2 / 3", with six columns and four on screen) is between the arrows only when some column is off screen. The columns are all in one row that slides behind a window as wide as the ones on screen: the ones off screen are `inert`, out of reach of the mouse, the keyboard and screen readers, and one that comes back is as it was left, its hours where they were; when the width crosses 650 px or 320 px the first column on screen stays, as far as the last ones allow. A finger that slides sideways over the columns moves their row with it, never past the first column or the last (`ColumnStrip`), and when it lifts the row settles on a column: one for each column the finger passed, the next counting once the finger is more than a quarter of the way to it. Over the columns, a row with the year of the days on screen, a second year at its end when they reach into it, and between them the options counter (`OptionCounter`), since every time is an option: at 60 a day that has times takes no more. Each column is a group named after its day for screen readers ("sábado, 20 de junio", a range "lunes, 22 de junio a martes, 23 de junio"), and the headers are hidden from them. Below 650 px the lists show the hours that leave the step's whole card above the bottom of the screen with the page at its top (`screenRoom` down to `STEP_CARD`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`), the same in the columns off screen; the place of a column's trash is left out of that room (`aside`), so it never takes an hour from the lists. Without a mouse (`TOUCH_QUERY`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, read once) a row over the step's arrows holds the button that shows the "+" of every marked time on screen (`SaveOffer`, whose `shown` is the `saving` of each `HourColumn` on screen). While a saved time is edited on a column (`editingSaved`), the step tells the others (`elsewhere`). A date of its own lists its times once each, marked and saved, by when they start and then by when they end, or "Sin horario" without any; a range of days or a saved range is one option, "Sin horario". Each option is drawn as the voting page draws it (`_time-blocks.scss`, `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`), under its day (`ColumnHeader`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`), and a column longer than the voting page shows (`foldOf`, `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`) scrolls inside its box under ︿ ﹀ (`ScrollFold`), which the keyboard reaches by the box itself. Each column is a list named after its day for screen readers ("lunes, 6 de julio"), and a time range is heard as "20:00–22:00". The step's two round arrows move the columns one at a time, on and on while held (`StepPager` with `held`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`): "Volver a los horarios" with the first columns on screen and "Día anterior" after that, "Día siguiente" and, with the last columns on screen, "Siguiente". It opens on the columns the times showed (`place`). Over the columns, the year of the days on screen, a second year when they reach into it, and between them the options counter (`OptionCounter`), as on the steps before it. A column of a date of its own is corrected in place: pressed anywhere but on its ︿ ﹀, or with its "Editar" (a button over the middle of its boxes, heard with its day, "Editar lunes, 6 de julio", shown with its boxes darker by the mouse over the column and by the keyboard's focus), it becomes the times step's column of its day (`HourColumn`, a group named after its day that takes the focus), among the others as they were, and what is corrected there goes to `selection` at once (`withTimes`). At the end of its trash's row a check ("Listo") closes it; Escape closes it too, both giving the focus back to its "Editar", though while a saved time is edited from its pill (`editingSaved`) the first Escape only undoes that edit, as the column does; a press anywhere outside it closes it, and opens the column pressed if it is one; the step's arrows and a finger's slide close it as they move the columns. What was corrected stays however it closes: the check and the moves end an edit going on first (`HourColumn.settle`). Below 650 px its hours are those that leave the card on screen (`screenRoom` down to `STEP_CARD`), and without a mouse the row of `SaveOffer` is over the arrows while a column is open. A range of days is never opened. | **`SavedPills`** (`saved-pills.*`), with `SavedPill`                                                                                                                           | `pills` (`SavedPill[]`: each with its `item`, what it stands for, and the `removeTitle` and `removeName` of its "−"; a page may give its pills more fields), `touch` (the device has no mouse; `false` by default), `stacked` (one under another; `false` by default), `removable` (the pills have their "−"; `true` by default, and a page takes it from all of them while an edit of its own goes on elsewhere), `pinned` (model, an item or `null`) → `previewed` (an item, or `null`), `removed` (an item); as content, one `<ng-template let-pill>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Saved items of any kind as a list of pills in a white well. What a pill reads is the page's: its template is drawn inside each pill with that pill (`WizardDates` gives the first and the last day of a saved range, "Lun 22 ➔ Vie 26"). Items are told apart by identity. A press on a pill sets `pinned` to its item, and on the pinned pill to `null`; `previewed` tells the item whose pill the mouse comes over, and `null` when it leaves (a finger's touch is not a mouse resting). A pill's "−", a small round button on its corner with the tip and the name its pill gives ("Eliminar el rango del 17 de junio al 19 de junio"), fires `removed`: the page deletes the item by taking its pill out of `pills`, and the focus then goes to the pill that takes its place, or to the last one (with no pill left, or while the page keeps the item, it is left alone). A page that gives a new item for the one of a pill, which is then another pill, calls `focus(item)`: the focus goes to the pill of that item once the list is drawn with it, and stays where it is when no pill is of that item. With a mouse or the keyboard every pill has its "−" while none is pinned, shown under the mouse or with the focus on the pill; with `touch` only the pinned pill has it, always shown. Side by side the pills are round and wrap into rows, larger and further apart with `touch`; `stacked` they are square, one under another and as wide as the list, which shows four at most and scrolls the rest under ︿ ﹀ (`ScrollFold`, held with `HoldRepeat`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`; they are there only while it scrolls), and a pill that joins the list is brought into sight (`reveal`). | **`ClearButton`** (`clear-button.*`)                                                                                                                                           | `label` (required: the trash's name, for screen readers and for the mouse resting on it), `question` (required: what it asks in its place) → `cleared`; signal `asking()` (its question is on screen)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | A trash that asks before it clears: the calendar's ("Limpiar selección", "¿Borrar todo?") and each day's column of the times step ("Limpiar horarios", "¿Borrar?"). Pressed, with a mouse, a finger or the keyboard, it gives way to its question in its place, with "Sí" and "No", the focus on "No", so a slip never loses what it clears: only "Sí" fires `cleared`, and the page clears; "No" and Escape bring the trash back with the focus, and a press anywhere else on the page takes the question away, what was pressed doing what it does. The question is heard once, as the name of its answers' group; the two answers go under it, together, where there is no room for them beside it. The page places it, and may shift its icon along the line (`--clear-trash-shift`). The page shows the component only while there is something to clear. | **`SaveOffer`** (`save-offer.*`), class `tools` on its element                                                                                                                 | `savable` (required: some marked time on screen has a "+" to show), `times` (the signal of the step's `DateSelection` itself, read at each change, so that a press that changes the times on its way down has changed them before the button hears it) → signal `shown()` (the "+" of every marked time on screen are shown)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Without a mouse, the row over the step's arrows of `WizardTimes`, and of `WizardSummary` while a column is open, as tall with its button as without: at its end, while `savable`, the button "Guardar una hora" that shows the "+" (each `HourColumn`'s `saving`), the first of the step taking the focus, and gives way to them; they hide, and the button is back, when the times change (as when one is saved), with a click anywhere but on a "+" or the button, and with Escape, which gives the button the focus back.                                                |
| **`HourColumn`** (`hour-column.*`)                                                                                                                                             | `times` (model, the `DayTimes` of one day), `full` (a signal the column reads at each change, never a value told before it: the event takes no more options; its answer can turn within one press, when the edit that press ends is an option more or another column has just taken the last one), `room` (the most its list may measure in pixels, or `null`), `touch` (the device has no mouse: a pill is pressed to find its "−"), `saving` (the "+" of every marked time is shown), `elsewhere` (a saved time of another column is being edited: no pill here has its "−" nor outlines its time under the mouse; `false` by default) → `editingSaved` (the edit of a saved time begins, `true`, or ends, `false`; once each, also when the edit goes from one pill to another); content: what the page puts at the end of the trash's row (the check of `WizardSummary`); method `settle()` (an edit of a saved time going on ends with its time, as a press elsewhere ends it: for the page that takes the column away) | The times of one day of the times step: a list of the 24 hours that shows 9, opens on the day's first time, marked or saved, at its top as far as the list goes (read once, as the column is made; at 9:00 without times), with the Tab stop there, and scrolls the rest under ︿ ﹀ (`ScrollFold`, held with `HoldRepeat`; with less `room` the hours that fit, 4 at least); once a scroll is let go it rests on a whole hour, the next one the way it was going. A press on an hour marks a time on the hour and a press on a marked time unmarks it (`pressHour`); the two arrows of a marked time move it a quarter within its hour (`shiftQuarter`), beside its band with a mouse and at the two ends of the band with a finger. A drag from an hour to another makes a time range (`RowDrag`, `dragHours`), drawn in a stronger green as it goes and made when it is released: a mouse presses and moves; a finger rests half a second on the hour (`LongPress`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`), a ring closing on its row meanwhile, drawn over the whole page so that the window on the columns of `WizardTimes` never cuts it (`HoldRing`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`), and then slides, while a finger that slides before scrolls the list. A range is one band with no gaps, its two ends with their minutes and arrows and the hours between small and faint; dragged from an end it moves that end, and a press on any of its hours removes it. One Tab stop: ↑ ↓ go along the hours, Enter or Space mark, ← → move the time; Shift+Enter holds an hour as an end of a range, drawn to the hour the focus goes to, Enter there makes it, Escape lets go, and leaving the column makes what is drawn; a tip over the hour held (`Tooltip`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) asks for the last hour, with the same words for screen readers ("Elige la última hora del rango."). While a range is being made, by a drag or from an hour held, no time is pressed or moved and the arrows are greyed. With a mouse, an arrow that can move its time grows and darkens under the pointer, as ︿ ﹀ do. The list is the floor of a well whose shadow is drawn over it, so it falls on the bands that scroll under its top and its bottom; a day without times is grey, with a soft outline a little forward and its hours a little larger for being nearer, sinks into the well with its first time and comes back up when its last one is unmarked (`--q-duration-sink`, `apps/web/src/styles`). That shadow is always drawn apart from the list (`will-change`), as is the row of columns in `WizardTimes`: a phone otherwise drops it while the columns move sideways. Each hour's button is named by its time ("18:15"), and an hour of a range adds its range ("19:00, rango de 18:15 a 20:00"). The page names the day on the host. A marked time is saved with the "+" on the corner of its last hour (`saveTime`): its hours are free again under a pale band and the time waits in a pill under the list (`SavedPills`, stacked). A mouse shows the "+" of the time it comes to, never of the one it has just changed until it comes back; Tab goes from a time to its "+"; `saving` shows them all, in red. A "+" that is not shown still takes a pointer's press, which does nothing, so a press on its place never unmarks the time. The mouse over a pill frames its time on the hours. A press on a pill edits its saved time (`SavedEdit`): its hours show it as a band with its arrows, which move it by quarters (`shiftEdited`), as ← → do on those hours and on the pill of a time of its own, while the pill reads it and the list brings its hours into sight (`ScrollFold.reveal`); the day's times change only when the edit ends (`endEdit`), with another press on the pill, which leaves the focus on the pill of the time as it is then saved (`savedLike`, `SavedPills.focus`), a press on another hour, arrow or pill, a drag from another hour, a click off the column (heard on its way down, so the edit has ended before what was pressed does what it does) or the focus going elsewhere, and Escape undoes it. A pill's "−" deletes its time (`removeSavedTime`); after the last one the focus goes back to the hours. While the day has a time, marked or saved, a trash is centred under the column (`ClearButton`), in a place kept also while the day has none where the room of the list spares its 45 px (`ScrollFold.spare`) or has no limit, so that nothing under the columns moves when it comes: its "Sí" clears them all, lets go of an hour held for a range and ends an edit going on with its saved time, and the focus goes back to the hours. What the page puts inside the column goes at the end of that row, the trash then at its start, and hides while the trash asks.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| **`RowDrag`** directive (`row-drag.ts`), `[appRowDrag]`, exported to templates as `appRowDrag`                                                                                 | → `dragging` (`RowDragSpan` or `null`), `dropped` (`RowDragSpan`); method `rested(row)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | A drag along the rows of a list that scrolls, each child of the element a row known by its place (the first is 0). A mouse begins it by pressing a row and moving more than 8 px; a finger or a pen, which scroll the list when they move, by resting on the row pressed: the element's user tells when with `rested(row)`. `dragging` fires when the drag begins and each time the pointer gets to another row, `dropped` when it is released, and the click that follows is swallowed. The pointer is at the row of its height, wherever it is sideways, and at the row at the top or the bottom of the list when it is above or below it; within 34 px of either end, or past it, once it has moved that way since it was pressed, the list scrolls that way every `FOLLOW_MS`, faster the further (9 px at most), so the drag gets to every row. A finger's drag does not scroll the list otherwise. A list that rests on whole rows (scroll snap) does not while a drag lasts, and does again when it ends. A drag whose release is lost, or that the browser cancels, ends with `dragging` as `null`. | **`WizardFinish`** (`wizard-finish.*`), with `EventEnding`, `NO_ENDING`, `SentEvent`, `CodeWait`, `CreatedEvent`                                                                                        | `details` (required, `EventDetails`), `selection` (required, `DateSelection`), `step` (required: its own number, for its arrow back; its card is named "último paso"), `ending` (model, required, `EventEnding`: the deadline chosen or `null` for the suggested one, the note, anonymous voting and the email typed without a session, which the page holds; `NO_ENDING` before anything is asked), `sent` (model, required, `SentEvent`: the event as last sent and the management token it was sent with, which the page holds; `null` before a send), `code` (model, required, `CodeWait`: the sign-in code waited for, the email it went to, when another may be asked and whether the session had ended, which the page holds; `null` while the step asks its fields) → `back`, `sending` (`true` when the event or a code is sent, `false` when its answer has come), `created` (`CreatedEvent`: the participation ID and the deadline)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | The last step inside its card, one Signal Form over `ending`: the deadline (`DeadlineField`), the suggested one (`suggestedDeadline`, `packages/domain/src`) until the organizer chooses another, with an amber line under it when voting would close on or after the first date (`closesAfterFirstOption`, naming `firstOptionDay`, with its year when it is not today's); the note, which grows with its text and stops at `NOTE_MAX_LENGTH` characters as people see them (`CharacterLimit`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`), its count under it heard with the field; anonymous voting (`Switch`) with an ⓘ that opens what it means in a `Tooltip`, closed by the ⓘ, Escape or a press anywhere else; and the signed-in account's email (`Session`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) with "¿No eres tú? Cerrar sesión" (`Session.signOut`; the step stays, the email field focused, `sent` forgotten, and the toasts of the header's sign-out, `Toaster`), or without a session an email field with "Te enviaremos un código para confirmar tu email." under it. Then the round arrow back ("Volver al paso" and the step before) and the round "Crear" (✓), which sends the event with every option (`proposedOptions`), the browser's time zone and the deadline, and in `X-Management-Token` the token of `sent` when the event has not changed since, else a new one (`newCapabilityToken`, `apps/web/src/app/core/api`) (`createEventEndpoint`, `packages/contracts/src/management`, through `ApiClient`); meanwhile a spinner takes the ✓'s place, the fields are dimmed and off and the arrow back is off, and a second press sends nothing. Created, the token sent is kept in this browser (`ManagementTokens`) and `created` tells the page; any failure keeps everything and says so under the arrows with "Reintentar", which sends again with the focus on "Crear"; a token refused because another account's event holds it (`MANAGEMENT_TOKEN_TAKEN`) is not sent again. Without a session, "Crear" first checks the email by the API's rule (`signInCodeBodySchema`; one that is not an address is said so, nothing sent, until it changes) and sends it a code (`sendSignInCodeEndpoint`); a code not sent says why under the arrows (`FailureNotice`, the sign-in page's texts) until it is sent or the email changes (a limit of codes is the email's). The code step then takes the fields' place, without arrows: a notice when the session had ended, and `SignInCode` (`apps/web/src/app/features/sign-in`, `purpose` "create", step 1's name for a new account), whose "Cambiar email" comes back to the fields with the email focused and whose new codes update `code`; signed in, the event is sent as above. An event refused with `401` (the session ended) has `Session` ask again and, without a session, sends the account's email a code (`sessionExpired`). Signed in another way while a code is waited for, `code` is dropped. | **`DeadlineField`** (`deadline-field.*`)                                                                                                                                       | `value` (model, required: the deadline, `YYYY-MM-DD`); its label is its content                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | The deadline of an event, chosen with three selects in a row, its day, its month in words ("noviembre", `monthName` of `calendarWords`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) and its year, each a pill with a chevron, named together by its label (a group) and each by its part ("Día", "Mes", "Año"). | **`OptionCounter`** (`option-counter.*`)                                                                                                                                       | `selection` (`DateSelection`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | How many of the options an event takes (`MAX_OPTIONS`, 60, `packages/domain/src`) the dates and their times amount to (`optionCount`), once they are 50 or more: a small white pill ("57 de 60 opciones"; `count-pill`, `apps/web/src/styles`), red and bold at 60. A status always on the page, empty until then, so that screen readers hear its text when it comes. The dates step and the times step show it. Each date of its own, each range and each saved range is one option, and a press or a drag never leaves more than `MAX_OPTIONS`. `times` holds the times of the dates of their own, by date and in order | `pressHour(day, hour, full)`, `dragHours(day, from, to, full)`, `shiftQuarter(day, hour, by, full)`, `saveTime(day, time)`, `removeSavedTime(day, time)`, `shiftEdited(day, edit, hour, by, full)`, `endEdit(day, edit, full)`, `withTimeRead(day, time)`, `savedLike(day, time)`, `timesOf(day)`, `optionsOf(day)`, `markAt(times, hour)`, `DayTimes`, `NO_TIMES`, `SavedEdit`, `DayTime`, `HourMark`, `HourDrag` (`day-times.ts`) | The times of one day, a `DayTimes`: the `marked` ones, in order and one an hour at most, and the `saved` ones, by when each starts and then by when it ends, no two alike, which can share hours with each other and with the marked ones (`NO_TIMES`: none). Each is a `DayTime` as an option carries its time: one time (`{ kind: 'single', at: 'HH:MM' }`) or a time range (`{ kind: 'range', start, end }`, two hours of the day at least, so it ends after it starts). Each different time is one option of the event, a marked time like a saved one the same option, and a day without times is one option: `timesOf` gives the different times in order and `optionsOf` how many options they are. When the event takes no more options (`full`), no rule leaves the day with one more: it answers the very same value, so a day without times still takes its first, and a time like a saved one is still marked. `pressHour`: an hour (0 to 23) with no time gains one on the hour, an hour with a time loses it, and any hour of a range removes the range. `dragHours`: a drag from an hour to another, up or down, makes one range of its hours, taking in the times of their own on the way and stopping at the hour next to another range; a drag of one hour is a time of its own; from a time the range keeps its minutes at that end; from an end of a range it moves that end, the other staying and each with its minutes, also when the range turns around; it answers a `HourDrag` (`drawn`, the time or range it draws, and `times`, the day's times with it among the marked), or `null` from an hour between the ends of a range and when it would add an option to a `full` event. `shiftQuarter`: the marked time of an hour, or the end of a range at that hour, moves a quarter later (`by` 1) or earlier (-1) and stops at :00 and :45 (the very same value there, on an hour with no time and between the ends of a range). `saveTime`: a marked time is no longer marked and is one of the saved, unless one like it is there already (the very same value for a time that is not marked). `removeSavedTime`: the times without a saved one. A saved time is edited through a `SavedEdit` (the time as it is `saved` and as it is `now`), which changes nothing of the day until it ends: `shiftEdited` moves it by quarters as `shiftQuarter` does a marked time (the very same edit at its stop and at any other hour), and `endEdit` saves it as it is now, in its place among the saved or gone into the one it has become like (the very same value when it ends as it was saved, and when a `full` event would get one option more from it: the edit is undone); an edit dropped is undone. `withTimeRead`: a time read from a phrase joins the times of the day, marked on its hours while they are free; when one of them has a time marked before, whichever of the two is a range is saved, so a time of its own takes the hour of the range marked there, and of two of a kind, the one read later; the very same value when one like it is there, marked or saved, and `null` for a range the hours of a day cannot hold (one within a single hour, or that ends before it starts). `savedLike`: the saved time like a time, itself when it is one of them or the one with its ends, or `null` when none is; after an edit has ended, what its saved time now is. `markAt`: what an hour is among a list of times, a `HourMark`: its `part` (`free`, `single`, `start`, `end` or `between`), its `time` and the `minutes` it shows (":15"). |
| `firstMarked(selection)` (`date-selection.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | The first day marked, of its own or in a range (saved ranges are not marked); none while no day is marked.
