                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `markingOf(reading, answers, timed)`, `Marking`, `TimeNotHeld`, `TimeRead`, `HalfOfDay` (`reading.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | What the reading of a phrase (a `DateInterpretation`, `packages/contracts/src/date-interpretations`) leaves on the wizard's calendar, a `Marking`: the `selection` (a `DateSelection`), the dates and times chosen in place of those chosen before, and `notHeld`, the times left out, each once (a `TimeNotHeld`: the time range and the days it was said of, in the phrase's order), whose days are marked without them. Each option is taken in the phrase's order. A day is a date of its own and a range of days a range, unless they share a day: then the range is saved, whichever was said first, as is the later of two ranges that share one; a range said twice is taken once. The time of a day joins the day's times (`withTimeRead`); the one of a question, as the reading picked for it (`answers`, a `HalfOfDay` each, `morning` or `afternoon`, in the order of the reading's questions; with none yet, the day has no time from it); and a time the hours of a day cannot hold, one that ends on the next day among them, is left out and told. Times are asked for when the reading says the time of a day, also one that is left out or that it tells was not understood or ends too late, so the step that asks for it is there; otherwise they are as they were (`timed`). `TimeRead`: a time as a reading says it, one time or a range that may end on the next day.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `noticeLines(notices, notHeld, transloco)`, `NoticeLine` (`reading-words.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | The lines that tell what a reading could not mark as it was said, in the language of the page: its notices, in the phrase's order, and after them the times the hours of a day cannot hold (`notHeld`), each with its days. A `NoticeLine` has its `lead`, the days the line speaks of, said before it ("vie 19 jun"; `null` for a line about none), and its `text`, what it says after the days and their colon, or a sentence of its own. A line about days that are marked names them, in the order of the calendar and with what they share said once ("sáb 3, 10 y 17 oct"), or counts them from four on; a line about what gave no option quotes the organizer's words, as does a time that was said of no day.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `questionLines(questions, transloco)`, `QuestionLine`, `AnswerWords` (`reading-words.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | The questions a reading asks about times said without their half of the day, in its order and in the language of the page. A `QuestionLine` is a sentence in three pieces, so that the days can stand out: `before`, the time as the phrase said it up to its days ("«a las 8» del"), `days`, named as a notice names them ("sáb 20 jun", "sáb 3, 10 y 17 oct") or counted from four on ("4 fechas", after "«a las 8» de"), and `after` (": ¿mañana o tarde?"); and the two readings to pick from, `morning` and `afternoon`, each an `AnswerWords`: what its button shows (`shown`: "8:00 · mañana", "17:00 → 19:00 · tarde", "20:00 → 0:00 (+1) · tarde") and what screen readers hear in its place (`heard`: "de 17:00 a 19:00, tarde").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PhraseReader`, `PhraseRead` (`phrase-reader.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | A service whose `read(text)` reads the dates and times of a phrase in plain language: the API reads it (`ApiClient`, `apps/web/src/app/core/api`), with the browser's time zone, in which today is taken, and the region of the browser's language, which orders a date written in figures. Every phrase gets an answer, within ten seconds, and nothing is thrown, a `PhraseRead`: `read` with its `reading`; `limited` when the API takes no more phrases from this address for now; `failed` for anything else that left the phrase unread.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RowDragSpan`, `FOLLOW_MS` (`row-drag.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | A drag between two rows by their place in the list (`from`, the row pressed; `to`, the row the pointer is at); and how often the list scrolls while the pointer stays near one of its ends (16 ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `columnWindow(selection, first, visible)`, `TimeColumn`, `ColumnWindow` (`time-columns.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | The columns of the times step, the `visible` ones from the one at `first` (from 0) on screen. The columns are one for each date of its own (`kind: 'day'`, with its `times`, a `DayTimes`) and for each range and saved range (`kind: 'range'`, which has no times), by first day; on the same day the date of its own first, then the ranges from the shortest; each says whether it is `onScreen`. A `ColumnWindow` holds every one of the `columns`, the `years` of the days of the ones on screen in order, the place the `first` column on screen has among them all and how many `places` it can take (1 when every column fits): the screen is never left with room after the last column, so a `first` too far puts the last columns on screen and gives the place they start at.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `columnsOnScreen()` (`column-strip.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | How many columns a step of columns puts on screen at a time, as a signal that follows the width of the screen: 4 from 650 px, 3 below, and 2 under 320 px. Called where a component is built (the times step and the summary).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `today()` (`today.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Today's date as the person's own clock and time zone show it, as `YYYY-MM-DD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `WizardDraft`, `newDraft()`, `openingDraft()`, `keepDraft(draft)`, `forgetDraft()`, `lastStepOf(dates)` (`wizard-draft.ts`) | Everything the wizard holds (the step and whether step 3 shows the summary of the times, the name and the title, the dates, the smart calendar's phrase, the month on screen, the last step's `EventEnding`, the `SentEvent` and the `CodeWait`), kept in the tab's `sessionStorage` under one key stamped with a version, so that the page opened again in the same tab (a reload, or a visit to another page) comes back as it was left; another version, unreadable content or a refused storage open a new wizard (`newDraft`, on step 1 with nothing asked, the calendar on today's month). `openingDraft` makes the kept draft fit for today: the dates before today dropped (`withoutDatesBefore`), the month no earlier than today's, a chosen deadline before today back to the suggested one, and the step no further than it can be (step 1 while the name or the title is not valid, the dates step when no date is left, the last step when no date of its own is left to have times), and the code waited for only while that step is still the last one. `lastStepOf` is the number of the last step with these dates (4 after the times and their summary, else 3), for the page and for that rule. |

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, `apps/web/src/app/core/api`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`, `apps/web/src/app/core/i18n`, `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`, `apps/web/src/styles`, `packages/contracts/src/date-interpretations`, `packages/contracts/src/management`, `packages/domain/src`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`.

### `apps/web/src/app/features/manage`

**Summary:** The management page.

**Purpose:** The page the organizer opens with the management link: the event's title and state, the choice of its final date and its confirmation, the links to pass on, and the state of the emails it sends the organizer, with the retry of a failed one. The management token is taken from the link, kept per event in the browser (`ManagementTokens`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) until it signs out, and sent in a header, never left in the address bar. Deadline, closing and cancelling come later.

**Components:**

| Component                                                    | Inputs → outputs                          | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`ManagePage`** (`manage-page.*`), route `/manage/:eventId` | `eventId`                                 | Reads the event with `EventManagement`; shows the "gestión" corner label, the title, the state ("Votación abierta hasta el…", "Votación cerrada…") with how many voted, `FinalDate`, `EventLinks` and `EventEmails`; sends the confirmation `FinalDate` asks for and shows the event as it comes back (an answer that arrives after the page moved to another event is dropped), and shows the event `EventEmails` hands it. A browser whose link does not manage the event sees "No tienes acceso a este evento" (focus on its heading), a cancelled event the cancelled `VoteEnded` (`apps/web/src/app/features/vote`); only a failed request offers to try again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| **`FinalDate`** (`final-date.*`)                             | `event`, `sending`, `failure` → `confirm` | Until a date is confirmed: the options as radios in the event's order, the recommended ones marked "Más votada", the only recommended one already chosen (none with a tie or no votes, and the button waits for a choice; when the event is read again, an option chosen by hand stays chosen while it is still offered), "Hora de Madrid" when an option has a time and the browser is in another zone, the button that confirms and, under it, a failed send; while a rate limit lasts, "Hay demasiadas visitas desde tu red en este momento." above the button, held and counting the seconds down ("Confirmar fecha en 42 s"). The usual case (voting closed, a recommended option, votes in) is confirmed at once; otherwise a question (`Dialog`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) first says why it is unusual (nobody voted; nobody can attend any option; not the recommended option, naming them; voting will close) and that the confirmation is final. Once confirmed: the green card with the date in full (`optionInFull`), focused when the organizer has just confirmed. Links to the full results. |
| **`EventLinks`** (`event-links.*`)                           | `participationId`, `resultsId`            | The participation and results links without their protocol, each copied in full (the `Toaster` confirms); the results open from their row.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **`EventEmails`** (`event-emails.*`) | `eventId`, `event` → `changed` | "Emails del evento": a well per queued email ("Resumen del evento", "Votación cerrada") with its state: "Enviado el {date} a las {time}" in the event's time zone, "Enviando…", "Se enviará al cerrar la votación" (`scheduled`: the API says when an email waits for the closing) or "Envío fallido" with "Reintentar" ("Reintentando…" while it is sent; a retry that does not get through says so under the list). Every "Reintentar" waits while one retry is on its way; after it, the event goes to the page (`changed`) with the email as the API answers it, being sent, and the keyboard's focus goes to the email's state. Every 10 seconds while an email is being sent, it reads the event with `EventManagement` and hands it on (also refused or cancelled; nothing once it is gone or overtaken by a newer event). Hidden when the event has no email. |

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                   | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `EventManagement` (`event-management.ts`)                                | The organizer's access to the events this browser manages: `claim(eventId, token)` hands a management link's token to `ManagementTokens` (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`), which keeps it per event until the browser signs out (one cut short is ignored), `claims` counts the links this page took (a page showing an event reads it again when it grows), `read(eventId)` and `confirm(eventId, optionId)` answer a `Management` and `retry(eventId, type)` (a failed email tried once more) a `RetryAnswer`; they reject only when the request did not get through (network, server, rate limit). A confirmation refused because the event changed meanwhile (`409`, `400`) answers the event read again, as does a retry of an email that was no longer failed. |
| `Management` (`event-management.ts`)                                     | What the page can show: `managed` with the event, `refused`, or `cancelled` with the kept title.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `RetryAnswer` (`event-management.ts`) | What `retry` answers: `retried` with the email's new state, or else a `Management` (the event read again after a `409` or `400`, refused, cancelled). |
| `openManagementLink` (`management-link.ts`), guard of `/manage/:eventId` | Opens the management link: hands its token to `EventManagement` and opens the page without it in the address bar or the history; also when the link is pasted over its event's page (only the fragment changes), which then reads the event again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/core/api`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, `apps/web/src/app/features/results`, `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`, `packages/contracts/src/management`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`.

### `apps/web/src/app/features/my-events`

**Summary:** "Mis eventos", the signed-in account's events.

**Purpose:** The private page an account lands on after signing in: one card with a row per event, newest first, each opening its management page, where the session grants access without the management link. Each row shows the title and the state line of the management page ("Votación abierta hasta el…", the lock and "Votación cerrada el…"), or the lock and "Fecha confirmada". A long list scrolls inside the card, never taller than the screen under its title, and fades at its foot while rows wait below. With no events, "Aún no has creado ningún evento." and "Crear un evento" to the home page.

**Components:**

| Component                                                       | Inputs → outputs | What it does |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------ |
| **`MyEventsPage`** (`my-events-page.*`), route `/my-events`     | none             | Reads `GET /api/me/events` (`apiResource`, `apps/web/src/app/core/api`) and shows loading, the error with "Reintentar", the empty card or the list. Measures the list when it appears, on its scroll and on a window resize to fade its foot while rows wait below (a fraction of a pixel left counts as none); the state line is marked `data-strong-text`, so its bold date reads darker than the rest. When the API answers `401` (the session ended since the page opened) it asks `Session` (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) again, so the header shows "Entrar", and then opens the sign-in page, which comes back here, unless the user has left the page meanwhile; it shows loading, not the error. |

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/core/api`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`, `packages/contracts/src/management`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`.

### `apps/web/src/app/features/not-found`

**Summary:** Unknown address.

**Purpose:** The page of any address that is not a page of the app, such as a link cut short when copied.

**Components:**

| Component                                           | Inputs → outputs | What it does                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`NotFoundPage`** (`not-found-page.*`), route `**` | —                | The page around `NotFoundCard` (`apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`): says the page does not exist and links back home. |

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`.

### `apps/web/src/app/features/results`

**Summary:** The results page.

**Purpose:** The page opened with the results link: what the votes amount to as they stand now, provisional while voting is open and final after, with the buttons to share the link. Labels of dates are made here, in the active language, from the calendar values the API answers.

**Components:**

| Component                                                         | Inputs → outputs                       | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`ResultsPage`** (`results-page.*`), route `/results/:resultsId` | `resultsId`                            | Reads the results; under the event's title shows the confirmed date when there is one ("La fecha más votada es:" then introduces the winners), the sentence of the outcome, the winning dates (one to three; with one winner on a named event, its attendee list), "Hora de Madrid" when a date it names in full has a time and the browser is in another zone, the counts, the `ResultsChart` and `ShareLinks` (`apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`) for the link. A link of no results (`404`, `400` malformed, `403` the participation link) shows `NotFoundCard`; a cancelled event (`410`) the cancelled `VoteEnded` (`apps/web/src/app/features/vote`); only a failed request offers to try again. |
| **`ResultsChart`** (`results-chart.*`)                            | `winners`, `alternatives`, `voters`    | A legend of the three answers, then one bar per winner and, under their own heading, one per alternative: each answer as wide as its share of the voters (an answer nobody gave is left out); options with names offer their `NameList` under the bar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| **`NameList`** (`name-list.*`)                                    | `names`, `look` (`winner` or `option`) | Who answered an option, behind a "Lista de asistentes" toggle that starts closed: the preferred in bold, then the available, then, struck through, who cannot come; a screen reader hears the answer after each marked name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

**Exports:**

| Export                              | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OptionLabels` (`option-labels.ts`) | Names an option in the active language: `long(option)` the winning date in parts ("sábado 11 de julio, a las 9:30", its connecting words marked `minor` to be drawn smaller), `plain(option)` the same as text, `short(option)` the chart's label ("Sáb 11 Jul · 9:30"). The shapes are in the catalogue (`results.label.*`, connecting words between `<m>` marks). |
| `LabelPart` (`option-labels.ts`)    | A piece of a long label: its `text` and whether it is `minor`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/core/api`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`, `packages/contracts/src/results`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app`, `apps/web/src/app/features/manage`.

### `apps/web/src/app/features/sign-in`

**Summary:** The sign-in page.

**Purpose:** The page where an organizer signs in with a code sent to their email, without a password: first the email, then the code, in one card. Once signed in it gives its place to the page that asked for it, "Mis eventos" without one. Both steps share the stylesheet partial `_sign-in-step.scss` (heading, lead, field, the texts under it and the buttons that read as links).

**Components:**

| Component                                             | Inputs → outputs                                          | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`SignInPage`** (`sign-in-page.*`), route `/sign-in` | `returnTo` (from the address's query)                     | Asks for the email in a Signal Form, with the focus on its field; an email that is not an address by the contract (`signInCodeBodySchema`, `packages/contracts/src/auth`, the form's validator) is said so without sending, once the form is sent and until the email changes. Otherwise sends it a code (`POST /api/auth/sign-in-code`) and hands over to `SignInCode`; a code not sent, or too many codes with the time to wait, shows under the button (`FailureNotice`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`), which is off while sending or waiting. "Cambiar email" comes back to the email, kept as typed. Once signed in, opens `returnTarget(returnTo)` (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) in place of itself in the browser's history.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **`SignInCode`** (`sign-in-code.*`)                   | `email`, `resendAfterSeconds`, `purpose` (`sign-in` by default, or `create` inside the wizard's last step), `name` (optional: the name a new account takes) → `signedIn`, `changeEmail`, `codeSent` (the seconds until another code may be asked) | The step that signs in with the code emailed to `email`, inside the page's card: one field of a Signal Form (`autocomplete="one-time-code"`, focused, read-only while checked) that keeps the first 6 digits of whatever is typed, pasted ("482 915") or suggested by the device and sends `POST /api/auth/sign-in` (with `name`, if given) as soon as the sixth is in; on `200` it has `Session` (`apps/web/src/app/core/auth`) ask the API for the account again (`refresh`) and then emits `signedIn`, so the page told next finds the account already known. Shows each refusal: a wrong code with the tries left, or a code of no more use (expired, or too many wrong tries), which turns the field off until a new code is sent; a check that failed otherwise shows `FailureNotice` and "Reintentar", not while a limit of tries lasts. A new code can be asked once `resendAfterSeconds` have passed, counted down in its button (`secondsUntil`, `apps/web/src/app/core/api`), and starts the step again, emitting `codeSent`; "Cambiar email" emits `changeEmail`. Inside the wizard (`purpose` "create") its heading is an `h2` and the hint under the field says that the last digit creates the event. |

**Depends on:** `apps/web/src/app/core/api`, `apps/web/src/app/core/auth`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, `packages/contracts`.

**Used by:** `apps/web/src/app` (routes).

### `apps/web/src/app/features/vote`

**Summary:** The voting page.

**Purpose:** The page a participant opens with the participation link, from the welcome card to the thank-you screen, including editing an earlier vote from the same browser or from the private link. Each browser guards its votes with its own vote-edit tokens. The boxes of times of a column of step 3 are drawn by the Sass mixin `rules` of `_time-blocks.scss` (their heights are the units `foldOf` counts), which the wizard's summary (`apps/web/src/app/features/create`) draws its boxes with too.

**Components:**

| Component                                              | Inputs → outputs                                                                           | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`VotePage`** (`vote-page.*`), route `/vote/:eventId` | `eventId`                                                                                  | Reads the event and the vote this browser holds, walks the voter through the steps as a stack of cards, keeps the name and the answers while the voter goes back and forth, sends the vote and shows the thanks. Once voting has ended (closed, confirmed, cancelled, also when a send is refused for it) shows `VoteEnded` instead, and a link of no event the `NotFoundCard`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **`VoteWelcome`** (`vote-welcome.*`), step 1           | `event`, `name` (two-way), `sending`, `quickVoteFailure`, `notice` → `choose`, `quickVote` | Presents the event, asks for the name on named events (a Signal Form over `name` with `isValidVoterName`; the field stops at `VOTER_NAME_MAX_LENGTH` with `CharacterLimit`), says when this browser already voted or the private link holds no vote, and offers the two ways to vote: choose the dates, or the quick vote; under them, why the quick vote failed (`FailureNotice`), both held while a rate limit lasts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **`VoteDays`** (`vote-days.*`), step 2                 | `options`, `answers` (two-way) → `back`, `done`, `toTimes`                                 | The proposed days month by month (the calendar's words from `calendarWords`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`, its card and grid from the `calendar` mixins, `apps/web/src/styles`); a tap toggles available ↔ unavailable, a long press or double-click marks the preferred one. In an event with times a day stands for all its times and goes on to step 3, and the mouse over a day (or a long press) previews its times. Options that share days are only outlined and answered by one chip each, with the same gestures. From 650 px the chips are below the calendar: the mouse or the keyboard focus on a chip paints its days, and an outlined day lifts its chips under the mouse. Below 650 px a tap on an outlined day opens its overlap in a `Dialog`: the overlap's days as a strip (`stripOf`) and its chips, the last one answered painted in the strip. |
| **`VoteTimes`** (`vote-times.*`), step 3               | `options`, `answers` (two-way), `timeZone` → `back`, `done`                                | The times of each day still possible, page by page (four columns from 650 px, three below, each narrower when the card leaves less room, two of their usual width under 320 px; back on the first page when that changes), answered the same way as the days; names the event's time zone when the browser's is another; each column under its header (`ColumnHeader`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`); a column over 10 units scrolls under ︿ ﹀ (`ScrollFold`), and on a phone any column too tall for the step's buttons to stay on screen (`screenRoom`); on a phone a tap on such a column's header or arrows opens its whole day in a modal (`Dialog`), answered the same way.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **`VoteConfirm`** (`vote-confirm.*`)                   | `answers`, `deadline`, `sending`, `failure` → `back`, `send`                               | The confirmation bubble over the last step: what the answers amount to, and the button that sends them; a failed send is told inside it (`FailureNotice`), the button held while a rate limit lasts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| **`VoteThanks`** (`vote-thanks.*`)                     | `organizerName`, `deadline`, `editLink`, `updated`                                         | The thank-you screen, with the private link to edit the vote from another browser, to open or copy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **`VoteEnded`** (`vote-ended.*`)                       | `ended`                                                                                    | The card in place of the steps once voting has ended: closed (white, with a notice about this browser's vote), confirmed (green, the date with weekday, year and time, the event's zone when the browser is in another, and the notice of a vote sent too late) or cancelled (pink, the kept title); takes the focus to its heading.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                                      | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MyVotes` (`my-votes.ts`)                                                                   | What this browser knows of its votes, one vote-edit token per event kept in the browser's storage and shared by its tabs. `stored(eventId)` reads the vote it holds (never fails: what the API cannot answer is `unread`, which voting takes as no vote); `cast(eventId, vote)` sends a new vote or edits the stored one and answers the private link and whether it replaced a vote; `claim(eventId, token)` takes the token of a private link just opened. |
| `StoredVote`, `CastVote` (`my-votes.ts`)                                                    | What `stored` and `cast` answer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `Ended`, `ClosedNotice`, `LateNotice` (`vote-ended.ts`)                                     | Why voting has ended (`closed`, `confirmed` with its option, `cancelled` with only the title), and what the closed card adds: `voted`, or a `LateNotice`, which the confirmed card can add too: `lateVote` (a new vote refused), `lateEdit` (an edit refused, the earlier vote kept).                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `VoteNotice` (`vote-welcome.ts`)                                                            | What the welcome card tells before the vote: `alreadyVoted` (this browser voted) or `unknownLink` (the private link holds no vote).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `openMyVoteLink` (`my-vote-link.ts`), route `/vote/:eventId/my-vote`                        | Opens the private link: hands its token to `MyVotes` and goes to the voting page, without the token in the address bar or the history.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `Answers` (`answers.ts`)                                                                    | The answer to each option, by option ID, while the vote is being made.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `allAvailable(optionIds)` (`answers.ts`)                                                    | Where a new vote starts: every option available.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `resumed(optionIds, stored)` (`answers.ts`)                                                 | Where an edit starts: the stored answers, `available` for options added since, without options the event no longer has.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `toggled(answers, optionId)` (`answers.ts`)                                                 | A tap: available ↔ unavailable; the preferred option goes back to available.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `withPreferred(answers, optionId)` (`answers.ts`)                                           | A long press or double-click: the option becomes the preferred one and the previous one goes back to available.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `dayState(answers, optionIds)` (`answers.ts`)                                               | In an event with times, what a day shows: unavailable when all its times are, available otherwise.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `dayToggled(answers, optionIds)` (`answers.ts`)                                             | A tap on such a day: all its times unavailable, or all available again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `calendarMonths(options)`, `CalendarMonth`, `CalendarDay` (`calendar-months.ts`)            | The months of step 2: every month with a proposed day, Monday first, with ranges drawn as one shape cut at week and month ends. Options that share days are drawn as one outline (`CalendarDay.overlaps`: the choices on the day; `CalendarDay.overlap`: the whole `Overlap`, its choices and span), and each month lists the chips of the overlaps it touches (`CalendarMonth.chips`).                                                                      |
| `DateChoice`, `DayGrid`, `shapeIn(grid, choice)`, `stripOf(overlap)` (`calendar-months.ts`) | What one tap answers (a date with all its times, or a range), days laid out seven to a row (a month is one), the shape a choice's days take in a grid (`Shape`, `apps/web/src/app/shared/ui`), to paint a chip's days, and an overlap's days on their own as the modal's strip.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `timePages(…)`, `TimePage`, `TimeColumn`, `TimeBlock` (`time-pages.ts`)                     | The pages of step 3: one column per day (or range of days) still possible, by first day and then by last day (a day before the ranges that start on it, from the shortest, as the wizard's times step orders them), its options in the event's order, a few columns per page; each column has a `key` (its days) and says how many blocks show before it scrolls (`fold`, from `foldOf`).                                                                    |
| `foldOf(times)` (`time-pages.ts`)                                                           | How many of a column's boxes, one per time in order, show before it scrolls (`null` when they all do): those that fit in 10 units, a time 1, a range of times 2, the whole day 3, as tall as they are drawn; a column of 11 units that ends in a time still shows whole. The wizard's summary folds its columns with it.                                                                                                                                     |
| `hourPreview(times)`, `HourPreview` (`hour-preview.ts`)                                     | What the preview over a day of step 2 lists: its first four times (`09:00`, `09:00 → 11:00`, `null` for the whole day), each once, and how many more there are.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `longDate(isoDate, locale, timeZone)` (`display.ts`) | `2026-07-01` → "1 de julio de 2026"; an instant is named by its day in `timeZone` (UTC when none is given, as a date-only string is read). |
| `clockAt(instant, locale, timeZone)` (`display.ts`) | `2026-11-22T09:14:00Z` in Madrid → "10:14": the time of an instant in `timeZone`, midnight as "0:00". |
| `fullDate(isoDate, locale)` (`display.ts`)                                                  | `2026-07-10` → "viernes 10 de julio de 2026".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `clockLabel(time)` (`display.ts`)                                                           | `09:30` → "9:30".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
