An option is a day or a range of days with a time (`single`, `range` with `nextDay` when it ends on the day after, `question` with the place of its question in `questions`) or `null` for whole days. A question is `{ fragment, dates, morning, afternoon }`: both readings of a time whose half of the day was not said. A notice is `{ kind, fragment, dates }` for `timeUnclear` and `tooLate`, the same with `written` (`weekday` or `date`) for `dayNotWritten`, `{ kind, fragment? }` for `unclear`, `mismatch`, `past` and `horizon`, `{ kind, fragment }` for `pastTime` (a time of today already past), and `{ kind }` for `tooMany`. |

**Depends on:** `packages/contracts/src`.

**Used by:** `apps/api/src/modules/date-interpretations`.

### `packages/contracts/src/health`

**Summary:** The health endpoint.

**Purpose:** `GET /api/health`: whether the API and its database answer.

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                                              | What it does                          |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| `healthEndpoint` (`health.ts`)                                                                      | The endpoint: `200` up or `503` down. |
| `healthOkSchema`, `healthUnavailableSchema`, `healthResponseSchema`, `HealthResponse` (`health.ts`) | Its two answers.                      |

**Depends on:** `packages/contracts/src`.

**Used by:** `apps/api/src/modules/health`.

### `packages/contracts/src/management`

**Summary:** Creating an event, the endpoints opened with a management token, and the signed-in account's events.

**Purpose:** Creating an event with the session (`POST /api/events`, not rate-limited of its own), and what the organizer can do with the management link, the token sent in `X-Management-Token` (22 to 64 base64url characters; optional, since the session will be the other way in). Reading the event to manage it, with the facts a decision needs (how many voted, which options are recommended), confirming its final option and retrying a failed email to the organizer. Rate-limited with the voting endpoints; `401` without a credential, `403` with one that does not manage the event (an unknown event too, so no `404`), `410` for a cancelled event.

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                                 | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `managedEventEndpoint` (`managed-event.ts`)                                            | `GET /api/events/{id}`: `200`, `400`, `401`, `403`, `410`, `429`, `500`.                                                                                                                                    |
| `managedEventSchema`, `ManagedEvent` (`managed-event.ts`)                              | The event as its organizer manages it: title, time zone, deadline, current state, options, confirmed option, `voters`, `recommendedOptionIds`, the participation and results IDs and `emails`, the emails queued to the organizer, the summary first (one never queued is not listed). |
| `emailTypeSchema`, `EmailType`, `emailStateSchema`, `EmailState` (`managed-event.ts`) | An email's type (`summary`, `voting-closed`) and state: `{ type, status: 'sent', sentAt }`, otherwise `{ type, status: 'scheduled' \| 'pending' \| 'failed' }` (`scheduled`: "voting closed" waiting for voting to close).                        |
| `managementHeadersSchema`, `managementTokenSchema` (`managed-event.ts`)                | The token header and its format.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `managementForbiddenSchema` (`managed-event.ts`)                                       | `403 FORBIDDEN`: the credential does not manage this event.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `myEventsEndpoint` (`my-events.ts`) | `GET /api/me/events`, with the session alone: `200`, `401`, `500`. |
| `myEventsSchema`, `myEventSchema`, `MyEvent` (`my-events.ts`) | "Mis eventos": the account's events, newest first, each with its participation ID (`id`), title, current state, deadline and creation instant. |
| `confirmOptionEndpoint` (`confirm-option.ts`)                                          | `POST /api/events/{id}/confirmation`: `200` with the managed event (also for the confirmed option sent again), `400`, `401`, `403`, `409`, `410`, `429`, `500`.                                             |
| `confirmOptionBodySchema`, `ConfirmOptionBody` (`confirm-option.ts`)                   | The body: `{ optionId }`, a UUID.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `confirmationRejectedSchema` (`confirm-option.ts`)                                     | `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`: a broken part of the request (`INVALID_FIELD`) or an option that is not the event's (`OPTION_UNKNOWN` with its ID).                                                                |
| `alreadyConfirmedSchema` (`confirm-option.ts`)                                         | `409 ALREADY_CONFIRMED`: another option is confirmed; a confirmation is final.                                                                                                                              |
| `retryEmailEndpoint` (`retry-email.ts`) | `POST /api/events/{id}/emails/{type}/retry`, no body: `200` with the email's state (pending), `400`, `401`, `403`, `409`, `410`, `429`, `500`. |
| `emailNotFailedSchema` (`retry-email.ts`) | `409 EMAIL_NOT_FAILED`: the email was sent, is still being tried, or was never queued. |
| `createEventEndpoint` (`create-event.ts`)                                              | `POST /api/events` with the page's management token in `X-Management-Token`: `201` (also for a repeated send of the account), `400`, `401`, `403`, `500`.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `createEventBodySchema`, `CreateEventBody`, `createEventHeadersSchema`, `proposedOptionSchema` (`create-event.ts`) | The body: `{ title, organizerName, timeZone, deadline, note?, anonymous, options[] }`, each option without an ID; no email, the session's account gives it. The headers: the management token the page created.                                                 |
| `eventCreatedSchema`, `EventCreated` (`create-event.ts`)                               | `201 { participationId, resultsId }`: the pages build the three links from them and the token they sent.                                                                                                           |
| `eventRejectedSchema`, `EventRejectedBody`, `EventProblem` (`create-event.ts`)         | `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`: every problem in the order of the wizard's steps (an option's with its `index` as sent, `DEADLINE_PAST`, `NOTE_TOO_LONG`), `INVALID_FIELD` for a header or body that breaks the contract, or alone `MANAGEMENT_TOKEN_TAKEN` (the token holds an event of another account). |

**Depends on:** `packages/contracts/src`, `packages/contracts/src/public-events`.

**Used by:** `apps/api/src/modules/events`, `apps/api/src/modules/management`, `apps/web/src/app/features/create`, `apps/web/src/app/features/manage`, `apps/web/src/app/features/my-events`.

### `packages/contracts/src/public-events`

**Summary:** Endpoints opened with a participation link.

**Purpose:** What a participant can do with the link: read the event, cast a vote, read and edit their own vote with its vote-edit token. All of them are rate-limited and answer `404` for an unknown link and `410` for a cancelled event.

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                                                                                                            | What it does                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `publicEventEndpoint` (`public-event.ts`)                                                                                                                         | `GET /api/public/events/{id}`: the event as participants see it.                                                             |
| `publicEventSchema`, `PublicEvent`, `optionSchema`, `Option` (`public-event.ts`)                                                                                  | The event (never the organizer's email, tokens, counts or votes) and its options.                                            |
| `eventNotFoundSchema`, `eventCancelledSchema` (`public-event.ts`)                                                                                                 | `404 NOT_FOUND`; `410 EVENT_CANCELLED` with the title.                                                                       |
| `castVoteEndpoint` (`cast-vote.ts`)                                                                                                                               | `POST /api/public/events/{id}/participations` with `X-Vote-Token`: stores a vote; a repeated token gets the same answer.     |
| `castVoteBodySchema`, `CastVoteBody`, `castVoteHeadersSchema`, `voteCastSchema`, `VoteCast`, `voteStateSchema`, `voteTokenSchema` (`cast-vote.ts`)                | The vote sent (name and one state per option, the new token in `X-Vote-Token`), its answer, the states and the token format. |
| `voteProblemSchema`, `VoteProblemDetail`, `voteRuleProblemSchemas`, `voteRejectedSchema`, `votingClosedSchema` (`cast-vote.ts`)                                   | Why a vote was refused: `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` with every problem, `409 VOTING_CLOSED`.                                     |
| `myVoteEndpoint` (`my-vote.ts`)                                                                                                                                   | `GET …/participations/mine`: the vote stored under the token.                                                                |
| `editVoteEndpoint` (`my-vote.ts`)                                                                                                                                 | `PUT …/participations/mine`: replaces it.                                                                                    |

### `packages/contracts/src/results`

**Summary:** The results endpoint.

**Purpose:** What the results link answers: the winners and their alternatives (never a full ranking), the counts, who answered each shown option each way on named events, whether the results are final, and the confirmed option apart. Rate-limited with the voting endpoints.

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                            | What it does                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `resultsEndpoint` (`results.ts`)                                                  | `GET /api/results/{resultsId}`: `200`, `400` malformed link, `403` participation link, `404`, `410`, `429`. |
| `resultsSchema`, `Results`, `resultsOptionSchema`, `ResultsOption` (`results.ts`) | The results and one shown option with its counts and, on named events, its `names`.                         |
| `resultsForbiddenSchema` (`results.ts`)                                           | `403 FORBIDDEN`: a participation link opens no results.                                                     |

**Depends on:** `packages/contracts/src`, `packages/contracts/src/public-events`.

**Used by:** `apps/api/src/modules/results`, `apps/web/src/app/features/results`.

### `packages/date-reader`

**Summary:** Reads dates written in plain language (`@qando/date-reader`).

**Purpose:** Turns what an organizer types to say when an event could take place ("todos los lunes de julio a las 20:30") into the date and time options to review, in Spanish, Catalan and English. It reads by rules alone, as a pure function with no input or output: no library, no service to call, and the same phrase on the same day always gives the same answer. The two limits of an event's options, how many and how far ahead, are the domain's (`packages/domain`), so the reader never proposes what an event would refuse. Its evaluation set runs with the unit tests and fails them below the agreed score, 100 %. It also judges whether a phrase copy-edited by someone else still says when as the original does (the edit check), with the same words and tokens it reads with. The API uses the edit check; nothing calls the reader yet.

**Contents:** `src/`; package, TypeScript, Vitest and Stryker settings (mutation threshold 85 %; the word lists of the languages are data and are not mutated; CI runs it in three parallel parts, `STRYKER_PART`: the two halves of `src/phrase-reader.ts` and the other files).

**Depends on:** `packages/domain`.

**Used by:** `apps/api`.

### `packages/date-reader/src`

**Summary:** The reader, its grammar and calendar, the check of an edit, and the evaluation set.

**Purpose:** The package has two public functions, exported by `index.ts`: **`readDates`**, with the types of its answer, and **`keepsWhen`**, the check of an edit; everything else is internal to the package and may change without notice. A phrase is read in three steps: the tokenizer (`tokenizer.ts`) cuts it into tokens with the vocabulary; the grammar (`phrase-reader.ts`) turns the tokens into a reading, what the phrase names as said, with no calendar arithmetic; the calendar (`calendar.ts`) turns the reading into options counted from today, with the questions and the notices; the reading keeps where each fragment starts in the phrase, which is what the order of the answer goes by. The grammar never names a word, so a new synonym or language is rows in `vocabulary/` and a case in the evaluation set. The reading rules (`R1`…`R34`) are written in `evaluation-set.json`, and the comments of the code name the one they apply. The check of an edit (`edit-check.ts`) compares how two phrases are written, the organizer's and its copy edit, with the tokenizer and the vocabulary, and never works out a date; the words it needs beyond the grammar's are data of each language too.

**Contents:** `vocabulary/`; the files below; `evaluation-set.json`, the 687 cases of the evaluation set with the reading rules, kept as written (Prettier leaves it alone); `evaluation-set.test.ts`, which runs every case and fails below 100 %, and checks that the options of every answer pass the domain's `validateOptions` (all but those whose time is still a question or ends on the day after).

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `readDates(text, { today, now, region })` (`read-dates.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | **The public interface.** Reads a date phrase written on `today` (YYYY-MM-DD, the day every relative date counts from) and answers `{ options, questions, notices }`, each in the phrase's order: the options to mark, one question per distinct time whose half of the day was not said, and one notice per fragment, every one that applies. `now` (HH:MM, optional) is the time of day: the earlier times of today are past, not marked and told (`pastTime`), and a bare hour whose morning has passed is its afternoon, with no question. `region` (the browser's, optional) makes a date in figures month/day for "US" and day/month otherwise. Every phrase gets an answer, never an error. It never guesses: what it cannot read is told in a notice with its fragment and never becomes an option, and so is a date that does not exist, is past or is more than 12 months away. A phrase that adds up to more than 60 options, or of which nothing is understood, marks nothing and gives one notice alone; one whose only content does not fit, is past or is too far gives the notice of each fragment. A day said twice is one option, and a whole day gives way to the same day with a time.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `keepsWhen(original, edited)` (`edit-check.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | **Public: the check of an edit.** Whether a copy-edited phrase still says when as the phrase the organizer wrote; `false` means the edit may read as other dates or times and must be discarded. The edit writes no number the original did not, counted as many times as written there (a number in words is the same number in figures; "00" minutes and leading zeros add nothing; "a esa misma hora" lets it write an hour again); an edit that drops a day of the month or an hour still says one of its kind, as a correction does ("el sábado a las 10, no, a las 11" → "el sábado a las 11" passes); the weekdays keep their order and none is new (a weekend said alone is never split into its two days); the only month named stays, and no month is new ("julio o agosto" → "junio o agosto" is discarded); no "today", "tomorrow" or "day after tomorrow" is new ("tomorrow at 8" → "today at 8" is discarded); a date negation stays or goes with the date it rules out; a week or month reference stays unless every day left has its day number; a half of the day is still settled when the original settles one, and the edit settles none the original does not ("Friday at 8" → "Friday at 8pm" is discarded, so the reader asks which half), says a half of no more hours than the times the original settles it, and gives no hour another half than the one said beside it ("Friday at 8am or Saturday at 9pm" → "Friday at 8pm or Saturday at 9am" is discarded); each meal word keeps a word of the same meaning. Only the original is read as a person reads it: a number word or a weekday with a typo, glued, split or shortened counts as written, and so does a half word with a typo, glued, split or in one of its listed short forms ("noxe", "porlatarde", "medio dia", "mñn"), though never a word the vocabulary already reads as something else ("mañana" alone is tomorrow); a month word with a typo and the month of a feast say their month, and a day word in a listed short form, with a typo or glued says its day ("mñn", "pasao mañna", "lunchtmrw"). It needs no "today" and never throws. |
| `DatesRead`, `ReadOption`, `ReadDates`, `ReadTime`, `ClockTime`, `TimeQuestion`, `ReadNotice`, `NoticeKind` (`dates-read.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                           | **Public:** the types of the answer. Dates are calendar values (YYYY-MM-DD) and times clock values (HH:MM), with no zone. An option is a day or a range of days with a time (`single`, `range` with `nextDay` when it ends on the day after, `question` with the place of its question) or none. A question is `{ fragment, dates, morning, afternoon }`. A notice is `{ kind, fragment, dates }` for `timeUnclear` and `tooLate` (`dates`: the first day of each option the fragment speaks of; none for a vague time said of no day), the same with `written` (`weekday` or `date`: how the phrase wrote the day) for `dayNotWritten`, `{ kind, fragment? }` for `unclear`, `mismatch`, `past` and `horizon` (no fragment when it speaks of the whole phrase), `{ kind, fragment }` for `pastTime` (a time of today already past; `past` is a date) and `{ kind }` for `tooMany`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `read(text, region)` (`phrase-reader.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Internal: the grammar. Reads a phrase into its reading.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `resolve(reading, today, now)` (`calendar.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Internal: the calendar. The answer a reading gives on a day: its options, questions and notices in the phrase's order; all the arithmetic and every rule that needs "today".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `tokenize(text, region)`, `plain(word)`, `nearlyEqual(a, b)`, `isYear(year)`, `Token`, `TokenType` (`tokenizer.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                     | Internal: the tokens of a phrase in order, each with its place in it; `isYear` says whether a number can be the year of a date (2000 to 2100; a year in figures outside them is read as the nearest); a word of four letters or more that is one letter away from a word of the vocabulary of five or more is read as that word, and a figure glued to letters is one word when the vocabulary lists it so ("2nite"). `plain` writes a word as the vocabulary does, in lower case and without accents; `nearlyEqual` says whether two words are the same or one letter apart (one changed, added, dropped, or two neighbours swapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `Reading`, `Item`, `Fragment`, `Expr`, `DayExpr`, `WeekdayExpr`, `RangeExpr`, `WeekExpr`, `WeekendExpr`, `WeekendsExpr`, `PatternExpr`, `NthExpr`, `NthEachMonthExpr`, `DayRef`, `MonthRef`, `Period`, `Nth`, `Time`, `TimeAt`, `TimeRange`, `TimeUnreadable`, `Clock`, `Half`, `Except` (`reading.ts`) | Internal: the shape of a reading. Its items, each a date expression (a day, a weekday, a range, a week, a weekend or several, a weekday pattern, the nth weekday of a month) with its times and the days left out. A `Fragment` is words of the phrase with where they start in it (`offset`): every item, time, vague time and run of words not understood is one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `known(value)` (`known.ts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Internal: a value the grammar has just checked is there; a missing one is a programming error, thrown as a `TypeError`, never an answer to a phrase.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

**Depends on:** `packages/date-reader/src/vocabulary`, `packages/domain/src` (`MAX_OPTIONS`, `latestOptionDate`).

**Used by:** `apps/api/src/modules/date-interpretations`, `apps/api/src/phrase-editor`.

### `packages/date-reader/src/vocabulary`

**Summary:** The words of each language.

**Purpose:** Every word the date reader knows, as data: one file per language, each row a term and the phrases that mean it, written in lower case, without accents and with single spaces. `vocabulary.ts` merges them into the one lookup the tokenizer uses. Internal to the package. The order of the rows matters to a mistyped word one letter away from several words, which is read as the first of them: the lookup keeps the phrases by kind of term, then language by language as written.

**Exports:**

| Export                                         | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `vocabulary()` (`vocabulary.ts`)               | Every phrase of every language with the term it means, built on first use and kept. A phrase listed in several languages means the same in each.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `wordsThatAreNotTypos()` (`vocabulary.ts`)     | The words that look like a typo of a vocabulary word and are not ("vienes" is not "viernes"), never read as one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `chatShorthand()` (`vocabulary.ts`)            | The short forms of chat of every language, each with the word the tokenizer reads it as ("xa" is "para", "tb" is "también"), built on first use and kept.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `editWords()` (`vocabulary.ts`)                | The words of the check of an edit, those of every language together, built on first use and kept.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `EditWords` (`term.ts`)                        | The words the check of an edit reads beyond the grammar's terms, as lists: minutes said in words with their number ("y media"), the word that joins tens and units ("treinta y uno") or a ten and its units ("diez y ocho"), what a person reads as a number where it stands ("veinti cinco", the Catalan "el u"), what chat glues before a month ("doct"), the words that after "no" correct the phrase ("no, perdón"), those that deny a date as "no" does ("tampoco"), the ways of saying one cannot ("can't"), the words of a span ("todo julio") the words that say a half of the day, each with its half and the short ways chat writes it ("mañana": "mñn"), the words that say a day counted from today, each with its short forms ("tomorrow": "tmrw", "tmr"), and the words that alone say the day after once tomorrow is said ("mañana y si no pasado"). |
| `Term`, `TermType`, `Language` (`term.ts`)     | What a phrase means to the grammar: its kind (a weekday, a month, a number, "every", "except"…) and what that kind needs (a value, the half of the day…); the words of one language, as rows of a term and its phrases, with its words of the check of an edit (`edits`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `es` (`es.ts`), `ca` (`ca.ts`), `en` (`en.ts`) | The words of Spanish, Catalan and English.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `shared` (`shared.ts`)                         | What the three languages write the same way: symbols (`&`, `+`, `@`) and short forms (`h`, `am`, `pm`, `ok`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

**Depends on:** `packages/date-reader/src` (the type of the half of the day, `reading.ts`).

**Used by:** `packages/date-reader/src`.

### `packages/domain`

**Summary:** Business rules (`@qando/domain`).

**Purpose:** The rules the API and the web must apply the same way, as pure functions with no input or output. The API enforces them; the web uses them to tell the participant about a problem before sending.

**Contents:** `src/`; package, TypeScript, Vitest and Stryker settings (mutation threshold 85 %).

**Depends on:** —

**Used by:** `apps/api`, `apps/web`, `packages/date-reader`.

### `packages/domain/src`

**Summary:** Event details, option rules, event state, vote rules and results.

**Purpose:** What makes the name and title of a new event valid, which lists of options it can have (how many, how far ahead, which ranges and times, none alike) and their order, what makes a whole new event valid, when an event accepts votes, what makes a vote valid, and what the votes amount to. `index.ts` re-exports `event-details.ts`, `options.ts`, `event-state.ts`, `vote.ts`, `results.ts` and `new-event.ts`.

**Exports:**

| Export                                                                                                           | What it does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `detailsProblems(details)`, `organizerNameProblem(name)` (`event-details.ts`)                                                                  | Every problem of an organizer's name and an event's title, the name's first, each measured once trimmed: missing (empty or only spaces), name too short (one character) or too long, title too long; none when an event can be created with them; `organizerNameProblem` is the name's alone, also the rule of the name a new account takes when signing in.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ORGANIZER_NAME_MIN_LENGTH`, `ORGANIZER_NAME_MAX_LENGTH`, `EVENT_TITLE_MAX_LENGTH` (`event-details.ts`)          | The limits, 2 and 30 for the name, 50 for the title.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `EventDetails`, `DetailsProblem` (`event-details.ts`)                                                            | The name and title as typed, and each problem's code.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MAX_OPTIONS` (`options.ts`)                                                                                     | The most options an event can have, 60; each date, range of dates or time proposed is one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `latestOptionDate(today)` (`options.ts`)                                                                         | The last day an event created on `today` can propose: the same day twelve months later (28 February after a 29 February); both as `YYYY-MM-DD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `validateOptions(options, today)` (`options.ts`)                                                                 | Checks the options proposed for an event on `today` (a calendar date in the event's time zone) and returns either the options in the order every page and email shows them or every problem at once, those of the list first (`NO_OPTIONS`, `TOO_MANY_OPTIONS` above 60), then each option's with its `index` in the list: a date before today (`DATE_PAST`) or after `latestOptionDate` (`DATE_TOO_FAR`), a range of dates of fewer than two days (`DATE_RANGE_DEGENERATE`), a time on a range of dates (`TIME_ON_DATE_RANGE`), minutes other than 00, 15, 30 and 45 (`TIME_OFF_QUARTER`), a time range that does not end after it starts (`TIME_RANGE_DEGENERATE`), the whole day of a date that another option gives a time (`WHOLE_DAY_WITH_TIMES`) and an option like one before it (`DUPLICATE`). Options may overlap. The order: by first day, then by when they start, an option without a time counting from 0:00; a day or a time before a range that starts with it; ranges of days by their last day, then time ranges by their end. Dates and times must be well formed (`YYYY-MM-DD`, `HH:MM`). |
| `ProposedOption`, `OptionDates`, `OptionTime`, `OptionProblem`, `OptionsValidation` (`options.ts`)               | An option as the organizer proposes it, with no ID: its dates (one date, or a range with both ends included) and its time (one time, a time range of the same day, or `null` for the whole day or days); each problem's code; and the answer of the check.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `clipBefore(text, limit, caret)` (`characters.ts`)                                                               | The text after an edit that left the caret at `caret`, as a field that stops at `limit` characters as people see them would leave it: the characters just before the caret (what was just typed or pasted) dropped past the limit, never one cut in two, the spaces around the text not counted, the text after the caret kept; and where the caret goes. Cuts a whole pasted document at once.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `countCharacters(text)` (`characters.ts`)                                                                        | Characters as people see them (an emoji or a letter with a combining accent is one), for the length rules and the counters of the fields that follow them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `currentState(event, now)` (`event-state.ts`)                                                                    | The event's state at `now`: an open event whose closing instant has come is closed, whatever is stored.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `closingInstant(deadline, timeZone)` (`event-state.ts`)                                                          | The instant voting closes: the last millisecond of the deadline's day in the event's time zone, also on days when the clocks change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `isValidVoterName(name)` (`vote.ts`)                                                                             | Whether a named event accepts the name: 2 to 30 characters once trimmed, counting characters as people see them (an emoji is one).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `sameVoterName(stored, typed)` (`vote.ts`)                                                                       | Whether a typed name is the voter of a stored vote, ignoring case and spaces.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `summaryOf(votes)` (`vote.ts`)                                                                                   | What the answers amount to before sending: an option preferred, some available but none preferred, or none possible.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `VOTER_NAME_MIN_LENGTH`, `VOTER_NAME_MAX_LENGTH` (`vote.ts`)                                                     | The name limits, 2 and 30.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `VoteState`, `VotingEvent`, `VoteInput`, `ValidVote`, `VoteProblem`, `VoteValidation`, `VoteSummary` (`vote.ts`) | The types of the rules' inputs and answers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `resultsOf(optionIds, votes)` (`results.ts`)                                                                     | The results of an event's votes: each option's preferred, available and unavailable count; the winners (best attendance = preferred + available, then most preferred, in the event's order); the alternatives (as well attended, less preferred); how many can and cannot attend; the outcome that picks the page's sentence (`no-votes`, `nobody-can-attend`, `one`, `two`, `three`, `several`, `all`) and whether the winners are listed (one to three). An unanswered option counts as unavailable.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `Results`, `ResultsOutcome`, `OptionTally` (`results.ts`)                                                        | The types of the results.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `validateNewEvent(input, now)` (`new-event.ts`)                                                                  | Checks a whole new event sent at `now`, today taken in its time zone, and returns either the event ready to store (texts trimmed, options in order, its closing instant) or every problem at once, in the order of the wizard's steps: name and title, options, a deadline before today (`DEADLINE_PAST`; today is valid), a note too long (`NOTE_TOO_LONG`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NOTE_MAX_LENGTH` (`new-event.ts`)                                                                               | The longest note, 150 characters.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `suggestedDeadline(options, today)` (`new-event.ts`)                                                             | The deadline the wizard suggests on `today` for those options: a week ahead, but the day before the first day proposed when that comes sooner, so voting ends before any option begins; never before today.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `closesAfterFirstOption(deadline, options)` (`new-event.ts`)                                                     | Whether voting, closing at the end of `deadline`, would still be open once the first option has begun: the deadline is that option's first day or later.
